questions_for_human: []
proposed_invariants:
- A write that updates the cached sale detail never carries `bill` over from a response
  that lacks it; any such write invalidates the detail's query instead.
- The sale bill renderer's switches over payment state and pricing rule are exhaustive,
  with no default branch.
- A `SaleDetail` in a test fixture or a mock always carries `bill`, as `null` or a
  whole bill, so no test builds a detail the API could not answer.
- The sale bill's reference cases include seller-typed names with `_` and `*` unchanged,
  and no renderer code rewrites a product or business name.
- The sale bill's button takes its loading state as an explicit value, never as a
  missing or `undefined` bill.
- The busy state of the sale bill's button after an assign clears only when the loaded
  detail shows a customer or its query errors, never on a timestamp.

subtasks:
  sale-detail-bill-data:
    id: sale-detail-bill-data
    title: Carry the sale bill on the front's sale detail and refetch it after assigning
      a customer
    brief: "Front only (`web/`). `GET /sales/{id}` already answers `bill` (see `SaleBillOut`,\
      \ `SaleBillLineOut`, `BillPaymentOut` in `app/sales/adapters/schemas.py`, and\
      \ `BillState`/`BillRule` in `app/sales/domain/sale_bill.py`). This subtask makes\
      \ the front carry it.\n\n1. **Types** in `web/src/api/sales.ts`:\n   - `BillState\
      \ = 'cash' | 'transfer' | 'paid' | 'owed' | 'none'`;\n   - `BillRule = 'bulk'\
      \ | 'gift'`;\n   - `SaleBillPayment { state: BillState; still_owed: number |\
      \ null }`;\n   - `SaleBillLine { product: string; quantity: GramsOut; amount:\
      \ number; rule: BillRule | null; unit_price: number | null }`;\n   - `SaleBill\
      \ { business_name; date; shipping_charged; total; lines: SaleBillLine[]; payment:\
      \ SaleBillPayment; balance: number | null }`, with `date` typed like `SaleDetail.date`.\n\
      \   - Add the required field `bill: SaleBill | null` to `SaleDetail`.\n   -\
      \ Keep `Sale['payment']` with `'owing'` as it is. The two vocabularies stay\
      \ apart on purpose.\n   - Export the key lists:\n     - `SALE_BILL_KEYS`, `SALE_BILL_LINE_KEYS`,\
      \ `SALE_BILL_PAYMENT_KEYS`, each declared `as const satisfies readonly (keyof\
      \ X)[]`;\n     - `BILL_STATES` and `BILL_RULES`, declared `as const satisfies\
      \ readonly BillState[]` and `readonly BillRule[]`.\n2. **Contract vitest.**\
      \ It reads `tests/fixtures/openapi_snapshot.json`. For the `SaleBillOut`, `SaleBillLineOut`\
      \ and `BillPaymentOut` schemas, it asserts that the property-name sets equal\
      \ the key lists, and that the `state` and `rule` enum sets equal `BILL_STATES`\
      \ and `BILL_RULES`. It asserts `bill` is a property of `SaleDetailOut`. The\
      \ snapshot is the truth: on a mismatch, the front is what changes.\n3. **Fixtures.**\
      \ Every `SaleDetail` in a test fixture or mock gets `bill`, as `null` or a whole\
      \ bill. That covers `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.test.tsx`, `VentaDetalle.sheets.test.tsx`,\
      \ `NuevaVenta.test.tsx`, `web/src/architecture/backCards.test.tsx`, and wherever\
      \ else `tsc` points.\n4. **Assign refetch.** In `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.tsx`,\
      \ `onCustomerChosen`:\n   - Remove the `queryClient.setQueryData<SaleDetail>(keys.sale(saleId),\
      \ ...)` spread of the `PUT` answer.\n   - Keep `invalidateResource(queryClient,\
      \ 'sales')`. It already refetches `keys.sale(saleId)`, because the key shares\
      \ the `sales` prefix.\n   - Keep `setAssigned(true)` straight after the `PUT`\
      \ succeeds.\n   - Rewrite the comment above it: the detail is now read back\
      \ from `GET`, because the bill's balance comes from the settlement pass only\
      \ that read does (ADR-0053).\n   - Change nothing else. The `isError`, offline,\
      \ generic-error and `GoneScreen` branches stay exactly as today, and so do the\
      \ assign error paths.\n\n**Acceptance:**\n- `tsc` strict, `oxlint` and vitest\
      \ pass, with nothing silenced.\n- Testing Library, `fetch` faked: a sale with\
      \ no customer and `bill.balance: null`. Assign a customer; the second `GET /sales/{id}`\
      \ answers with the customer. The screen shows the customer's name from that\
      \ second response, and two `GET`s were made.\n- The existing `VentaDetalle`\
      \ tests pass unchanged, apart from adding `bill` to their fixtures."
    seams:
    - GET /sales/{id} faked at fetch, through the VentaDetalle screen with Testing
      Library
    - tests/fixtures/openapi_snapshot.json against the exported key lists of web/src/api/sales.ts
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-376.sale-detail-bill-data

    reviewed_head: 6633429bc0c56e960be1524cfeb2caaa1c359327
    reason: 'Open important findings (final pass before merge, not a return):

      sample drifts through a local `sameSet` helper. The real contract assertions

      proof covers a copy of the comparison, not the comparison that guards the contract,
      and the docstring above `sameSet` says something untrue ("exported through the
      rejection test").

      Unit `sale-bill-button`:

      - web/src/components/Boton.tsx:51: Boton gained a free `className` prop for
      a one-off class (`sale-bill__boton`), against the component''s own header: no
      screen writes a one-off button class. The header was not updated.

      Unit `integration`:

      - web/src/components/Boton.tsx:52: Boton still takes a free className prop for

      - web/src/api/saleBillContract.test.ts:47: The drift-rejection test runs a local
      sameSet helper that the real contract assertions (toEqual on sorted arrays)

      docstring''s "exported through the rejection test" is untrue.

      Plan:

      - web/src/mobile-standard/statesSales.ts:75: The brief''s loading and copy-failed
      WalkState entries were dropped, and the docs were rewritten to say none are
      added, because §10''s offender lists cannot take a new screen name''s cells;
      this plan-versus-standard conflict needs a plan decision.'
    return_category: spec-missing-test
  sale-bill-renderer:
    id: sale-bill-renderer
    title: Format quantities from hundredths and render the sale bill's plain text
      from copy
    brief: "Pure TypeScript with no DOM, in `web/`. It imports the `SaleBill` types\
      \ from `web/src/api/sales.ts`.\n\n1. **`formatQuantity(hundredths: number):\
      \ string`** in `web/src/format/money.ts`.\n   - Integer arithmetic only: the\
      \ whole part `Math.trunc(h / 100)` with dot thousands, then, when the remainder\
      \ is non-zero, a comma and two digits with the trailing zero dropped.\n   -\
      \ Cases: 1 → \"0,01\", 10 → \"0,1\", 105 → \"1,05\", 350 → \"3,5\", 100000 →\
      \ \"1.000\", 0 → \"0\".\n   - `formatGrams(grams)` becomes `formatQuantity(Math.round(Number(grams)\
      \ * 100)) + ' g'`, with today's exact output. Its existing tests stay untouched;\
      \ add the `formatQuantity` cases to `money.test.ts`.\n2. **Copy** in `web/src/copy/sales.ts`:\
      \ a `saleBill` group, following the module's existing patterns and ADR-0049/0050.\
      \ Texts (from the Tech Writer):\n   - `share` \"Share\"; `copy` \"Copy\"; `copied`\
      \ \"Bill copied\";\n   - `shareFailed` \"Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste\
      \ it into the chat.\";\n   - `copyFailed` \"Couldn't copy. Select the text below\
      \ and copy it by hand.\";\n   - `blockName` \"Sale bill\";\n   - `header(business,\
      \ date)` → business, `\\n`, date;\n   - `item(product, quantity, unit, amount)`\
      \ → `{product} {quantity} {unit} · {amount}`;\n   - `bulk(product, quantity,\
      \ unit, unitPrice, amount)` → `{product} {quantity} {unit} · bulk price {unitPrice}/{unit}\
      \ · {amount}`;\n   - `gift(product, quantity, unit)` → `{product} {quantity}\
      \ {unit} · Gift · $ 0`;\n   - `shipping(amount)` → `Shipping · {amount}`; `total(amount)`\
      \ → `Total · {amount}`;\n   - `paidCash` \"Paid in cash\"; `paidTransfer` \"\
      Paid by transfer\"; `paid` \"Paid\";\n   - `stillOwed(amount)` → `Still owed\
      \ on this sale: {amount}`;\n   - `balance(amount)` → `Your balance: {amount}`;\
      \ `credit(amount)` → `In your favor: {amount}`;\n   - `unit` \"g\".\n   The\
      \ separator is \" · \". Regenerate `en-XA`, and update the baseline files the\
      \ copy module's staleness and limit tests require, with its scripts, never by\
      \ hand. Register the strings in `LIMITED_COPY` where the module's limits require\
      \ it.\n3. **`renderSaleBill(bill: SaleBill, text = SALE_BILL_TEXT): string`**\
      \ in `web/src/sales/saleBill.ts`. Pure; the copy group is injectable so a test\
      \ can override the unit. Output joined by `\\n`:\n   - the header, then an empty\
      \ line;\n   - one line per bill line: `rule === 'bulk'` uses `bulk` with `formatMoney(unit_price)`,\
      \ `'gift'` uses `gift`, `null` uses `item`;\n   - `shipping` only when `shipping_charged\
      \ > 0`;\n   - an empty line, then `total`;\n   - the payment line: `cash` →\
      \ paidCash, `transfer` → paidTransfer, `paid` → paid, `owed` → stillOwed(still_owed),\
      \ `none` → no line;\n   - the balance line: `balance > 0` → balance, `balance\
      \ < 0` → credit(magnitude), `0` or `null` → no line.\n   Formats:\n   - The\
      \ date is dd/mm with no year, using the app's existing date formatter.\n   -\
      \ Quantity is `hundredthsOfApiGrams(line.quantity)`, then `formatQuantity`,\
      \ then the copy's `unit`.\n   - Money is `formatMoney` (`$ 11.000`), never the\
      \ catalog's format.\n   - The `switch`es over `payment.state` and `rule` are\
      \ exhaustive, with no `default` and a `never` check.\n   - Names are never rewritten,\
      \ escaped or stripped.\n   - No literal word, no \"g\", no `formatGrams`.\n\
      4. **`web/src/sales/saleBill.cases.json`.** Each case is a `SaleBill` input\
      \ and its expected text, **written by hand against the PRD**, never produced\
      \ by running the renderer. Cases:\n   - with and without a bulk line; a VIP\
      \ line and a special line, which arrive as `rule: null` and read like list lines;\
      \ a gift line;\n   - a $0 gifts-only sale with a non-zero balance, using the\
      \ payment state the server derives;\n   - cash, transfer, paid later, partly\
      \ owed, fully owed, and `none`;\n   - a balance above $0, a credit, and a balance\
      \ of exactly $0;\n   - no customer (`balance: null`);\n   - with and without\
      \ shipping; one line and several lines;\n   - quantities 0.01, 0.1, 1.05 and\
      \ 1000;\n   - a variety named ``OG*Kush_`x` `` whose line shows the name unchanged.\n\
      \   A vitest runs every case and expects 0 mismatches.\n5. **Further tests:**\n\
      \   - With the copy's unit overridden to \"u\", a bulk line reads \"3,5 u\"\
      \ and \"/u\".\n   - **Leak test**, over fixtures whose names hold no `*`, `_`\
      \ or backtick. The text has no `*`, `_`, backtick, \"http\", \"VIP\", \"special\"\
      , \"list\", no note text or customer name planted in the fixture data, and no\
      \ id.\n   - **Source guard**: no `web/src/sales/saleBill*` or `web/src/screens/**/SaleBill*`\
      \ production source contains a `'g'` / `\"g\"` string literal or `formatGrams`.\n\
      \n**Acceptance:** `tsc` strict, `oxlint` and vitest pass. The existing `formatGrams`\
      \ tests are unchanged and green."
    seams:
    - formatQuantity and formatGrams in web/src/format/money.ts
    - renderSaleBill(bill, text) in web/src/sales/saleBill.ts
    - the saleBill copy group in web/src/copy/sales.ts

    reviewed_head: c32dda3fbc9992f607dc152b8df8ad3977769cbd
  sale-bill-button:
    id: sale-bill-button
    title: Share or copy the sale bill from the sale's detail
    brief: "Front only (`web/`). It imports `renderSaleBill` and the `saleBill` copy\
      \ group from the previous subtask, and `canShare`, `shareText` (`web/src/share.ts`)\
      \ and `copyText` (`web/src/clipboard.ts`) as they are.\n\n1. **`SaleBillButton`**\
      \ (new component, e.g. `web/src/screens/ventas/SaleBillButton.tsx`). Props,\
      \ both required:\n   - `state: { kind: 'loading' } | { kind: 'ready'; bill:\
      \ SaleBill | null }`\n   - `refreshing: boolean`\n   - `onCopied: () => void`,\
      \ so the screen shows the toast.\n   \n   **What it renders:**\n   - `ready`\
      \ with `bill: null` renders nothing.\n   - `loading`, or `ready` with `refreshing`,\
      \ renders the button with its label, `disabled` and `aria-busy=\"true\"`, with\
      \ no digit or `$`.\n   - Otherwise, a `secondary` `Boton` labelled Share when\
      \ `canShare() && !shareFailed`, else Copy.\n   \n   **Click handler:**\n   -\
      \ If the pending ref is set, it returns.\n   - Otherwise it sets the ref and\
      \ bumps a sequence ref, renders the text from the bill it holds, and calls `shareText(text)`\
      \ or `copyText(text)` **synchronously, with nothing awaited before the call**.\n\
      \   - It then awaits the outcome. Only if the sequence still matches does it\
      \ clear the ref and apply the outcome.\n   - A `visibilitychange` back to `visible`\
      \ while pending clears the ref. Add the listener at the tap; remove it when\
      \ the ref clears or on unmount.\n   - A new tap clears both failure lines.\n\
      \   \n   **Outcomes:**\n   - shared → nothing;\n   - cancelled → nothing;\n\
      \   - share failed → the `shareFailed` line with the attention icon, and `shareFailed=true`,\
      \ so the label becomes Copy;\n   - copied → `onCopied()`;\n   - copy failed\
      \ → the `copyFailed` line with the attention icon, and directly beneath it a\
      \ block `role=\"region\"` with `aria-label` from the copy's `blockName`, holding\
      \ the bill text in a `<pre>` with `white-space: pre-wrap` and `user-select:\
      \ text`.\n   \n   **Styles:**\n   - The button is at least 44 px, with `user-select:\
      \ none`, `touch-action: manipulation` and an `:active` state. Colours, type\
      \ and spacing come from tokens only.\n   - Nothing goes to the console or to\
      \ storage.\n2. **`VentaDetalle.tsx` wiring:**\n   - Put the button first in\
      \ `venta-detalle__acciones`, before \"Assign customer\" and the delete icon.\
      \ It is never inside the \"Sale saved\" notice.\n   - One notice state, `'assigned'\
      \ | 'copied' | null`, replaces `assigned`, through the existing `AvisoExito`\
      \ and timer. \"Customer assigned\" and \"Bill copied\" share that one slot.\n\
      \   - **`refreshing`** is true while `assignMutation.isPending`. After a successful\
      \ `PUT` it stays true until the loaded detail has `customer !== null`, or the\
      \ query errors. Never clear it on a timestamp. A focus refetch never sets it.\n\
      \   - **The pending branch** no longer returns a bare `Loading`. It renders\
      \ the same `main.pantalla > .cuerpo` with `aria-busy=\"true\"`: an empty title\
      \ row with no date, `Loading`, and the `venta-detalle__acciones` row holding\
      \ `<SaleBillButton state={{kind:'loading'}} refreshing={false} …/>`. It is laid\
      \ out where the row sits in the final layout, so `content-shift` stays within\
      \ `CONTENT_SHIFT_PX`.\n   - The error, offline and `GoneScreen` branches are\
      \ unchanged.\n3. **Tests** (Testing Library, `fetch` faked):\n   - Share calls\
      \ `navigator.share` with the rendered text.\n   - A share `AbortError` shows\
      \ nothing, and the label stays Share.\n   - A share failure shows the line with\
      \ the icon, and the label becomes Copy.\n   - With `isSecureContext=false`,\
      \ `document.execCommand('copy')` is called synchronously within the click, and\
      \ \"Bill copied\" shows.\n   - A copy failure shows the line and the block named\
      \ \"Sale bill\", holding the text.\n   - `bill: null` → no button.\n   - Loading\
      \ (`GET` held) → the button is disabled and `aria-busy`, with no digit or `$`.\n\
      \   - A double tap with the share held calls share once.\n   - With the share\
      \ held, `visibilitychange` to visible, then a new tap, calls share again. Then\
      \ the first promise rejects as failed and no failure line shows.\n   - After\
      \ an assign, the copied text ends with `Your balance: $ …` from the second `GET`.\n\
      \   - A stale focus refetch from before the assign, released after the `PUT`,\
      \ leaves the button busy until the detail with the customer arrives.\n4. **Mobile\
      \ check:**\n   - Add the `loading` state (`GET /sales/{id}` held) and the `copy-failed`\
      \ state (copy forced to fail in the walk's page) to `web/src/mobile-standard/statesSales.ts`\
      \ as `WalkState` entries.\n   - If the walk needs a small hook to make copy\
      \ fail, add it in the walk's support, never in production code paths.\n   -\
      \ Share-failed is covered by Testing Library only.\n   - The `mobile` project\
      \ of `npm --prefix web run e2e` passes with no new offender.\n   - Look at `VentaDetalle`'s\
      \ design comparison before delivering, and keep its `scripts/design/screens.json`\
      \ entry true.\n5. **Docs:** check `docs/product/sales.md`'s sale bill section\
      \ against what was built (placement, both failure lines with the attention icon,\
      \ the loading and no-bill behaviour), and correct only what differs."
    seams:
    - the VentaDetalle screen through Testing Library, with GET /sales/{id} and PUT
      faked at fetch and navigator.share / clipboard / execCommand stubbed at the
      browser API
    - SaleBillButton component props (state, refreshing, onCopied)
    - WalkState entries walked by web/e2e/mobile.spec.ts
    depends_on:
    - sale-bill-renderer
    status: approved
    screens:
    - VentaDetalle

invariants:
- The sale bill's payment state, still-owed amount and balance are read from the `bill`
  of the last GET /sales/{id}; nothing in web/ recomputes settlement.
- No write puts a sale detail into the query cache by hand (no setQueryData on keys.sale);
  a write that changes a sale invalidates it and the screen reads the refetch.
- Every SaleDetail in a test fixture or mock carries `bill`, as null or a whole bill.
- No fixed word of the sale bill is a literal outside web/src/copy/; the unit is read
  from the one keyed copy entry, and no saleBill* module holds a "g" literal or calls
  formatGrams.
- A sale bill quantity is formatted from integer hundredths (hundredthsOfApiGrams
  then formatQuantity), never from float arithmetic.
- The expected texts in saleBill.cases.json are written by hand against the PRD, never
  generated by running the renderer.
- No renderer code rewrites, escapes or strips a product or business name; the no-*,
  _ or backtick rule applies to the bill's fixed words only.
- The renderer's switches over payment state and pricing rule are exhaustive, with
  no default branch and a never check.
- Nothing of the sale bill is stored, logged or written to the console or browser
  storage; shareText or copyText is called synchronously in the click handler, with
  no await before it.
- One synchronous pending flag guards Share and Copy, and only the latest tap's outcome
  may clear it or change what the screen shows.
- SaleBillButton takes its loading state as an explicit required value, never as a
  missing or undefined bill.
- The sale detail's existing error, offline and not-found branches are unchanged.

- subtask: integration
  version: 1
  location: copy:WRITING_STANDARDS.md
  claim: 'The product has no `WRITING_STANDARDS.md`: its texts are written and reviewed
    with no criteria'
  severity: important
  scope: outside
  category: copy-criteria-written
  status: open
  proposed: '# Writing standards


    The texts a person reads in the app, in the bot, and in shared text such as the
    sale bill. The source language is English (`web/src/copy/`). `en-XA` is always
    regenerated from it, never written by hand.


    ## Voice and tone

    - Plain, calm and brief. Write as a helpful colleague talking to the seller, not
    as a system talking about itself.

    - Never blame the person. When something fails, say what failed, then what to
    do: "Couldn''t copy. Select the text below and copy it by hand."

    - No exclamation marks, no emoji, and no "please" or "sorry".

    - Use contractions where the speech is natural ("Couldn''t", "can''t").


    ## Register and person

    - Screen texts speak to the seller. Shared texts (the sale bill) speak to the
    customer, using "your": "Your balance", "In your favor".

    - Imperatives for actions; past participles for confirmations ("Bill copied",
    "Catalog saved", "Customer assigned").


    ## Vocabulary

    - `CONTEXT.md` (the glossary) is the source of truth. Use its "In the app" word,
    and never a word from its _Avoid_ list (e.g. never "invoice", "receipt" or "ticket"
    for the sale bill).

    - Use one word per concept everywhere. A text that names a button uses the button''s
    exact label ("Tap Copy").

    - Never hard-code one business''s way of working (Assume nothing). Units and channel
    names come from keyed entries, and no text names a chat app.


    ## Formats

    - Money: `$ 11.000` (formatMoney), never the catalog''s format.

    - Quantities: Argentine separators, trailing zeros dropped (0,01 · 0,1 · 1,05
    · 3,5 · 1.000), with the unit from its copy entry.

    - Dates in short texts: dd/mm.

    - Inline separator in compact lines: " · " (a middle dot with a space on each
    side).

    - Text meant to be shared is plain: no `*`, `_`, backtick or link in any fixed
    word. Data the seller typed goes out exactly as stored.


    ## Length limits and patterns by kind of text

    - **Button label:** 1 to 3 words, a verb first, with no object when the screen
    makes it clear ("Share", "Copy", "Delete sale"). It is also the accessible name
    when the icon is decorative.

    - **Toast (success):** 2 to 4 words, an object and a past participle, with no
    period ("Bill copied").

    - **Notice or failure line:** at most 80 characters. One sentence on what happened
    and one on what to do, each ending with a period.

    - **Help or empty line:** at most 80 characters, one sentence.

    - **Accessible name of a region:** a noun phrase in sentence case, with no verb
    ("Sale bill").

    - **Lines of a shared text:** `Label · value` or `Label: value`, in sentence case,
    one fact per line.


    ## Mechanics

    - Sentence case everywhere; no Title Case.

    - No trailing period on labels, toasts or accessible names. Periods on full sentences.

    - Every fixed word lives in `web/src/copy/`. No literal word in components or
    renderers.'

  claim: The Sale bill entry puts its screen word inline in running prose, not in
    the glossary's `_In the app_:` marker form that every other term uses, so the
    screen word is hard to find and reads differently from the other entries.
  severity: important
  scope: subtask
  category: copy-criteria-changed
  status: open
  proposed: 'The facts about one recorded sale that a seller may share with that sale''s
    customer: business name, date, lines, shipping charged, total, payment state and
    the customer''s current balance. They are worked out live from settlement when
    the sale''s detail is read and never stored, and the balance is the customer''s
    whole account at the moment of sharing. It is shared as plain text by Share or
    Copy on the sale''s detail. _In the app_: Sale bill (the accessible name of its
    selectable block); the action reads Share / Copy.'

  location: copy:4.2:web/src/copy/sales.ts:46
  claim: '`paidCash`, `paidTransfer`, `share` and `copy` point to entries in the Customers
    and common groups instead of holding their own text, so a later change to the
    seller''s wording elsewhere would silently change the bill the customer reads.'
  severity: observation

design_items:
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Main job, start: NuevaVenta → VentaDetalle with "Sale saved"'
  text: In the prototype, NuevaVenta's "Guardá la venta" links to Ventas.dc.html,
    not to VentaDetalle. sales.md (Saving) says the app opens the sale's detail, and
    the written definition governs. The flow's landing is right, but the prototype
    cannot walk the main moment and should be fixed in the drawing.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Main job, step 1: VentaDetalle, Share/Copy placed "below the notice"'
  text: '"Sale saved" is a toast that lasts a few seconds (sales.md; drawn as a toast
    in Estados "Avisos al guardar"), not a fixed block. "Below the notice" is not
    a position. The button''s place has to be fixed relative to the screen''s own
    content (total, lines, "Borrar venta"). Sin prototipo.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Main job, Copy path: "Bill copied" toast right after saving'
  text: The seller taps Copy while "Sale saved" is still up, so "Bill copied" arrives
    while that notice and its Undo are showing. The flow says the notice and Undo
    stay "untouched" but not whether the two toasts stack, queue or replace each other.
    If one replaces the other, Undo goes away early. "Delete sale" still recovers,
    so this is not a dead end, but the flow must say what happens.

  kind: observation
  step: Share failed → Copy; Copy failed → selectable block (VentaDetalle)

    specified.'

# Share or copy a sale's bill from the sale's detail

## Original request

Blocked by: #375

**What this delivers.** On the sale's detail (`VentaDetalle`), the screen the seller lands on after saving with the "Sale saved" notice, a Share/Copy button sends a short plain-text sale bill to the customer. It is built from the `bill` field that `GET /sales/{id}` already answers: `business_name`, `date`, `lines[{product, quantity, amount, rule bulk|gift|null, unit_price}]`, `shipping_charged`, `total`, `payment{state cash|transfer|paid|owed|none, still_owed}`, `balance`, or `bill: null`.

**1. `formatQuantity`** (`web/src/format/money.ts`)
- Takes integer hundredths with no float arithmetic: the whole part with dot thousands, then a comma and up to two decimals with trailing zeros dropped.
- `formatGrams` becomes `formatQuantity(Math.round(Number(grams) * 100)) + ' g'`, with exactly today's output. Its existing tests stay untouched.
- Cases: 1 → "0,01", 10 → "0,1", 105 → "1,05", 100000 → "1.000", 350 → "3,5".

**2. Copy** (`web/src/copy/sales.ts`, ADR-0049 and ADR-0050)
A `saleBill` group:
- Share; Copy
- "Bill copied"
- "Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste it into the chat."
- "Couldn't copy. Select the text below and copy it by hand."
- the block's accessible name "Sale bill"
- the header (business, a new line, dd/mm)
- item `{product} {quantity} {unit} · {amount}`
- bulk `{product} {quantity} {unit} · bulk price {unitPrice}/{unit} · {amount}`
- gift `{product} {quantity} {unit} · Gift · $ 0`
- `Shipping · {amount}`; `Total · {amount}`
- Paid in cash; Paid by transfer; Paid; `Still owed on this sale: {amount}`
- `Your balance: {amount}`; `In your favor: {amount}`
- `unit` = "g". It is the one unit entry until #318.

Regenerate `en-XA` as the copy module's staleness tests require.

**3. Renderer** (`web/src/sales/saleBill.ts`)
`renderSaleBill(bill, t)`, pure:
- the header; a blank line;
- the lines, then shipping only when above 0;
- a blank line; the total; the payment line (none for `none`); the balance line only when it is not null (a credit as its magnitude).

Quantity goes through `hundredthsOfApiGrams`, then `formatQuantity`, then `t(unit)`. Money uses `formatMoney` (`$ 11.000`), never the catalog's format. No literal word, no "g", and no `formatGrams`.

**4. Reference set** (`saleBill.cases.json`)
Every expected text is **written by hand against the PRD**, never generated. Cases:
- with and without bulk
- a VIP line and a special line reading like list lines
- a gift; a $0 gifts-only sale with a non-zero balance
- cash, transfer, paid later, partly owed, fully owed
- a balance; a credit
- no customer
- with and without shipping
- one line and several lines
- quantities 0.01, 0.1, 1.05, 1000

Expect 0 mismatches.

Further tests:
- Unit entry overridden to "u" → "3,5 u" and "/u".
- Leak and plain text: no note, customer name, "VIP", "special", "list", ids, "http", `*`, `_` or backtick.
- A guard that no `saleBill*` source has a "g" literal or `formatGrams`.
- A vitest that reads `tests/fixtures/openapi_snapshot.json`'s `SaleBillOut` schemas. It asserts their property names and enums equal the front's key lists, which are declared with `satisfies` against the `SaleBill` type in `web/src/api/sales.ts`.

**5. Button** (on `VentaDetalle`, never inside the "Sale saved" notice)
- **Label:** `canShare()` gives Share, otherwise Copy.
- **Click:** it renders and calls `shareText` or `copyText` synchronously, with no `await` first. That keeps the `execCommand` fallback inside the gesture over plain http (ADR-0002).
- **Outcomes:**
  - copied → the toast "Bill copied";
  - cancelled → nothing;
  - share failed → the share-failed line with the attention icon, and the label flips to Copy;
  - copy failed → the copy-failed line, with the text in a selectable block.
- `bill: null` hides the button.
- While loading, the button keeps its label, is disabled with `aria-busy="true"`, and shows no digits or `$`.
- A 404 takes the existing not-found path.
- At least 44 px, tokens only, `user-select: none`, `touch-action: manipulation`, an `:active` state.
- The text is always built from the last detail response. The detail keeps its existing invalidations and refetches on focus.
- Nothing goes to the console or to storage.

Testing Library tests (`fetch` faked):
- Share calls `shareText` with the text.
- With `isSecureContext=false`, `execCommand('copy')` is called synchronously right after the click, and the toast shows.
- cancel, share failed, copy failed, `bill` null, loading.

Add the copy-failed and loading states to `WalkState`. The mobile e2e project passes with no new offender.

**6. Docs**
`docs/product/sales.md` documents the job, where Share lives, the sale bill text and its absent lines, the unit entry, and the PRD's metrics.

############### Invariants

1. The sale bill's payment state, still-owed amount and customer balance come from the one settle() pass behind OwingSales.settlement_of, in the same response as the detail's still_owing; nothing in app/sales or web/ recomputes settlement.
2. SaleBillOut is closed (extra="forbid") and has no field for note, customer name or id, sale or line id, VIP flag, a pricing rule other than bulk or gift, cost, margin or link; the business comes from CurrentBusinessId only.
3. A failure to derive a sale bill makes `bill` null and never fails the rest of the sale's detail; its error message names the rule broken and no sale, customer or amount.
4. No fixed word of the sale bill is a literal outside web/src/copy/; the unit is read from the one keyed copy entry, and no sale bill module holds a "g" literal or calls formatGrams.
5. A sale bill quantity is formatted from integer hundredths, never from a float.
6. The expected texts of the sale bill reference set are written by hand against the PRD, never generated by running the renderer.
7. Nothing of the sale bill is stored, logged or written to the console or browser storage; the share or copy call runs synchronously in the click handler.

Part 2 of 2 of GH-322. The plan and its interview are in `.agentic/tasks/GH-322.md`; the original task branch `agentic/GH-322` is context for reading, nothing to bring in.

<!-- agentic-split: GH-322 part 2 -->

## PjM

_Reaped 1 background process(es): 1 term_

New visible Share/Copy button on the sale's detail that sends a plain-text sale bill to the customer. This is new user-facing behaviour, so the product docs change too.

## Interview: Product Analyst→PM

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: the job to be done

The `grilling` and `domain-modeling` skills could not be loaded in this session, so I am following the round format from the task. The interview file `.agentic/tasks/GH-322.md` is not in this worktree either. I am working from the request and the product knowledge above. Round 1 covers the job only. The PRD already has the bill's fields, the copy and the button states fixed, so I am not asking about those.

❓ **Q1** - **Situation**: In which moment does the seller reach for the bill? The request says it is on the sale's detail, the screen after "Sale saved". I see three possible moments:
- (a) right after saving, while the customer is still waiting in the Telegram or WhatsApp chat;
- (b) later, when the customer asks "how much do I owe?" or "what did I order?";
- (c) when the customer has paid part of a debt and the seller wants to show what is left.

➡️ Recommendation: (a) is the main situation and (b) is the second. Both are served by the one button on the detail, so nothing else is needed. The Jobs section of the brief says a sale is recorded "in under a minute". The bill should add one tap to that, not a new step.

❓ **Q2** - **Progress wanted**: What should happen once the seller taps the button? I propose the bill goes out in the same chat the order came from, with no retyping, so the customer sees what they were charged and what they still owe.
- Does the seller want the customer's balance in it, or only the sale?
- Is the bill for the customer only, never an invoice? The out-of-scope list already excludes "Invoices, taxes and accounting exports".

➡️ Recommendation: Job story: "When I have just recorded an order that came in over Telegram or WhatsApp, I want to send the customer a short plain-text bill of that sale (lines, total, how they paid, what they still owe) in one tap, so I can answer in the chat without retyping or working out the total again." The bill is facts only, a **Sale bill** in the glossary. It is not an invoice.

❓ **Q3** - **How the seller judges it went well**: Which measures count as success? I suggest three:
1. From "Sale saved" to the bill in the chat takes one tap, with no typing, on the phone.
2. The bill's total and balance always match the app, because both come from the same settlement. The customer never gets a figure the seller then has to correct.
3. Nothing private leaks: the bill has no note, no customer name, no VIP or special-price wording, and no ids or links.

➡️ Recommendation: Adopt these three. Number 2 has the `0 mismatches` reference set as its evidence and number 3 has the leak test. Number 1 is the measure the seller feels.

❓ **Q4** - **What they do today without it**: Is my understanding right that the seller retypes the order and the total into the chat by hand, or screenshots the sale's detail? Or do they not reply with figures at all, and the customer asks again later? The spreadsheet gave no way to produce a shareable text.

➡️ Recommendation: Record that today the seller retypes, or copies from the screen, and works out the balance by hand. That is the cost the button removes. If your answer is different, tell me what you actually see them do.

### Round 1: answers

### Round 1: the job to be done

**Q1**: Accepted. (a) is the main moment: the sale is saved and the customer is still waiting in the chat. (b) comes second: the customer asks later what they ordered or what they owe, and the seller opens that sale's detail and taps the same button. Both use the one button on the sale's detail. Nothing new is added to recording a sale, so the brief's "under a minute" is unaffected.

(c) is not this job. A payment is not a sale, and this bill describes one sale. Still, the balance line covers most of what (c) needs. The glossary defines the **Sale bill** balance as the customer's *current* whole account, worked out live when the detail is read. A bill shared after a later payment therefore already shows what is left. A separate payment receipt would add scope with no request behind it, so the answer is no.

The PRD should say plainly that the balance line is the balance at the moment of sharing, not the balance on the sale's date. In case (b) the two can differ.

**Q2**: Accepted, with one change to the wording.

- **The balance goes in.** The glossary's **Sale bill** already includes the customer's current balance, and a credit shows as "In your favor".
- **It is for the customer only and is never an invoice.** Invoices are out of scope in the brief.
- **The job story should not name Telegram or WhatsApp.** Criterion 4 (Assume nothing) says channels must not be hard-coded, so the story names "the chat" and not a particular app.

Job story: "When I have recorded a sale, or a customer later asks what they took or what they owe, I want to send them a short plain-text bill of that sale in one tap: its lines, shipping, total, how they paid, what is still owed on it and their balance now. Then I can answer in our chat without retyping or working out figures, and the customer sees the same numbers I do."

**Q3**: Accepted, with criterion 1 made exact and one criterion added.

1. **One tap, no typing.** On the sale's detail, one tap on the phone either opens the phone's share sheet with the bill text ready or puts it on the clipboard. Picking the chat and sending are the phone's own steps and do not count against the app. When sharing or copying fails, the seller still gets the text with no typing: they are told to tap Copy, or they get the text in a selectable block.
2. **The figures match the app.** The bill's total, payment state, still-owed amount and balance always agree with the app, because they come from the same settlement as the detail. The evidence is the hand-written reference set with 0 mismatches.
3. **Nothing private leaks.** The bill has no note, customer name, VIP, special or list wording, ids or links. The evidence is the leak test. A VIP or special-price line reads like a list line.
4. **Added: Assume nothing holds.** No fixed word, unit or format is hard-coded in the bill. The unit comes from the one keyed copy entry, and the evidence is the test that overrides it to "u". This keeps the bill right for a business that sells in other units, ahead of #318.

**Q4**: Accepted as an assumption. Assumption: today the seller retypes the order and total into the chat by hand, or sends a screenshot, and works out the customer's balance from memory or from the spreadsheet. The spreadsheet could not produce any text to share. A different answer here would not change what the PRD defines, so this does not go to the human.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: behaviour that serves the job

The job is settled and I am not reopening it. The request already fixes the fields, the copy, the button states and the failure paths. These five questions cover what it leaves open.

❓ **Q1** - **A balance of exactly $0**: The request says the balance line shows "only when it is not null" and a credit shows as its magnitude. It does not say what a customer with nothing owed sees. A customer who has just paid in full would read "Paid" and then "Your balance: $ 0".
➡️ Recommendation: Show no balance line at $0, so the bill ends at "Paid". Show "Your balance" only above $0 and "In your favor" only for a credit. Mention of debt then appears only when there is debt, and the customer gets no redundant line. If you prefer "Your balance: $ 0" as reassurance, say so and I will record it.

❓ **Q2** - **The date on the bill**: The header reads business name and dd/mm, with no year. In case (b) the seller may share a sale from months ago. Without a year, a bill for 03/02 could be read as the wrong year.
➡️ Recommendation: Keep dd/mm as specified. The customer is in the same chat and knows the order, and Argentine dates stay as they are ("Numeric and date formats stay Argentine", Product language). Adding the year would be a later change, not part of this job.

❓ **Q3** - **Seeing the text before it goes**: Today the text appears on screen only when copying fails. Otherwise the seller taps Share or Copy and the text goes straight to the share sheet or the clipboard.
➡️ Recommendation: Show no preview. A preview adds a step to a job that is meant to be one tap. The bill is facts only and built from what the seller already sees on the detail, and the phone's share sheet or the chat shows the text before sending.

❓ **Q4** - **A sale with no customer**: A sale paid in full on the spot to someone not registered still has a bill: its lines, total and "Paid in cash" or "Paid by transfer", with no balance line. The seller may well hand it to that buyer.
➡️ Recommendation: Show the button on these sales too, with no balance line, since there is no customer account to report. The button hides only when the server answers `bill: null`.

❓ **Q5** - **Feedback after a successful share**: A copy gives the toast "Bill copied". The request does not say what the seller sees after the share sheet completes. The phone's own sheet already closes or shows where it went, and a sale shared more than once gives no other way to tell.
➡️ Recommendation: Show nothing after a successful share. The sheet closing is the confirmation, and a second toast would only repeat it. A cancelled share also shows nothing, as in the request. The seller can tap again at any time, and no "already shared" state is stored, because the job leaves nothing stored.

### Round 2: answers

### Round 2: behaviour that serves the job

**Q1**: Accepted. At exactly $0 the bill has no balance line. "Your balance: {amount}" appears only when the balance is above $0, and "In your favor: {amount}" only for a credit, shown as its magnitude. This follows the job: the bill mentions debt only when there is some, and a customer who has paid in full sees the bill end at the payment line.

The PRD lists the balance line as absent in two cases: when the balance is null (a sale with no customer) and when it is $0. The reference set gets one case of a customer whose balance is $0.

**Q2**: Accepted. The header stays as business name and dd/mm, with no year. Product language keeps Argentine date formats. The customer reads the bill in the same chat as the order, so the year adds nothing for this job.

Assumption: a seller sharing a sale from an earlier year is rare enough that no year is needed. If it becomes a need, adding the year is a later copy change.

**Q3**: Accepted. There is no preview. It would add a step to a one-tap job. The phone's share sheet or the chat composer already shows the text before it is sent, and the bill holds only facts the seller sees on the sale's detail. The text appears in the app only on the copy-failed path, as the request says.

**Q4**: Accepted. A sale with no customer keeps the button. Its bill shows the lines, shipping when charged, the total and "Paid in cash" or "Paid by transfer", with no balance line because there is no customer account. Nothing on the bill names or stands in for the buyer, and the note stays out as the leak rule says.

The button hides only when the server answers `bill: null`. This case is already in the reference set ("no customer").

**Q5**: Accepted. A successful share shows nothing in the app, because the share sheet closing is the confirmation. A cancelled share also shows nothing. Only a copy gives the toast "Bill copied", since a clipboard gives no other sign it worked.

No "already shared" state is kept or shown. That respects the invariant that nothing of the sale bill is stored, and the seller can share the same sale again at any time.

### Closing

### Closing summary

**Job (settled in round 1).** "When I have recorded a sale, or a customer later asks what they took or what they owe, I want to send them a short plain-text bill of that sale in one tap, so I can answer in our chat without retyping or working out figures, and the customer sees the same numbers I do." The story names "the chat", not a channel, because of Assume nothing. It sits on the sale's detail and serves two moments: right after saving, and later when the customer asks. A payment receipt is not part of this job.

**Success criteria**
1. One tap on the phone reaches the share sheet or the clipboard, with no typing. If either fails, the text is still available with no typing.
2. The bill's figures always match the app's own settlement. Evidence: the hand-written reference set with 0 mismatches.
3. Nothing private leaks: no note, customer name, VIP, special or list wording, ids or links. Evidence: the leak test.
4. Assume nothing holds: the unit and every fixed word come from the copy module. Evidence: the test that overrides the unit to "u".

**Behaviour decided**
- **Balance line.** It shows only when the balance is above $0 ("Your balance") or a credit ("In your favor", as its magnitude). It is absent when the balance is null (a sale with no customer) and when it is $0. The reference set gets a $0-balance case.
- **Balance timing.** The balance is the customer's whole account at the moment of sharing, not on the sale's date. The PRD says so plainly.
- **Date.** The header shows the business name and dd/mm, with no year.
- **Preview.** None. The text appears in the app only on the copy-failed path.
- **Sale with no customer.** It keeps the button. The button hides only when `bill` is null.
- **Feedback.** A copy shows the toast "Bill copied". A successful share and a cancelled share show nothing. Nothing is stored, including any "already shared" state.

**Assumption recorded.** Today the seller retypes the order and total, or sends a screenshot, and works out the balance by hand. A different answer would not change the PRD.

**Nothing for the human.** No question is left open that the product knowledge or the PM could not settle. The request already fixes the fields, the copy, the failure states and the invariants.

## Product Analyst

_Reaped 1 background process(es): 1 term_

### Problem and job

When I have recorded a sale, or a customer later asks what they took or what they owe, I want to send them a short plain-text bill of that sale in one tap, so I can answer in our chat without retyping or working out figures, and the customer sees the same numbers I do.

The main moment is right after saving, with the customer still waiting in the chat. The second is later, when the customer asks, and the seller opens that sale's detail and taps the same button. Today the seller retypes the order and total, or sends a screenshot, and works out the balance by hand. This is an assumption. A different answer would not change what is defined here.

The story names "the chat", not a channel (Assume nothing).

### Scope

- On the sale's detail, a Share/Copy button sends the **Sale bill** of that sale. It is never inside the "Sale saved" notice.
- The bill is plain text and facts only. It is not an invoice, which stays out of scope in the brief.
- A payment receipt is not part of this job.
- Nothing about the bill is stored.

### Success criteria

1. **One tap, no typing.** One tap on the phone either opens the phone's share sheet with the bill ready or puts it on the clipboard. Choosing the chat and sending are the phone's own steps. If sharing or copying fails, the seller still gets the text without typing.
2. **Figures match the app.** The bill's total, payment state, still-owed amount and balance always agree with the sale's detail, because they come from the same settlement. Evidence: a reference set of bills with hand-written expected texts and 0 mismatches.
3. **Nothing private leaks.** The bill has no note, customer name, VIP, special or list wording, ids or links. A VIP or special line reads like a list line. Evidence: the leak test.
4. **Assume nothing holds.** Every fixed word and the unit come from the copy module. The unit is one entry, "g" by default, until #318. Evidence: with the unit set to "u", the text reads "3,5 u" and "/u".

### The bill text

In order:
1. The business name, then on a new line the date as dd/mm, with no year. A blank line follows.
2. One line per sale line:
   - a list, VIP or special line: `{product} {quantity} {unit} · {amount}`;
   - a bulk line: `{product} {quantity} {unit} · bulk price {unitPrice}/{unit} · {amount}`;
   - a gift: `{product} {quantity} {unit} · Gift · $ 0`.
3. `Shipping · {amount}`, only when shipping charged is above $0.
4. A blank line, then `Total · {amount}`.
5. The payment line:
   - "Paid in cash" or "Paid by transfer" for an on-the-spot payment;
   - "Paid" when the sale was paid in full later;
   - `Still owed on this sale: {amount}` when something is owed, partly or fully;
   - no line when the payment state is none.
6. The balance line:
   - `Your balance: {amount}` when the balance is above $0;
   - `In your favor: {amount}` for a credit, shown as its magnitude.

Formats:
- Quantities are in grams to the hundredth with Argentine separators, trailing zeros dropped: 0,01 · 0,1 · 1,05 · 3,5 · 1.000.
- Money reads `$ 11.000`, never the catalog's format.

Absent lines:
- Shipping is absent at $0.
- The payment line is absent when there is none.
- The balance line is absent when the sale has no customer and when the balance is exactly $0.

The balance is the customer's whole account at the moment of sharing, not on the sale's date. For a sale shared later the two can differ, and this is intended.

### Button behaviour

- **Label.** Share where the phone can share, otherwise Copy.
- **Share.**
  - Success shows nothing; the sheet closing is the confirmation.
  - A cancelled share shows nothing.
  - A failed share shows "Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste it into the chat." with the attention icon, and the label becomes Copy.
- **Copy.**
  - Success shows the toast "Bill copied".
  - A failed copy shows "Couldn't copy. Select the text below and copy it by hand." with the bill in a selectable block, accessible name "Sale bill".
- **No preview.** The text appears on screen only in the copy-failed case.
- **No bill.** The button is hidden when the server gives no bill. A sale with no customer keeps it, with no balance line.
- **Loading.** The button keeps its label, is disabled and busy, and shows no digits or `$`.
- **Not found.** A missing sale takes the existing not-found path.
- **Fresh text.** The text always reflects the latest detail the screen holds. The detail refreshes as it does today.
- **Phone.** The tap target is at least 44 px and the button works over the home network on plain http. It follows the design tokens and the mobile check, with no new offender.
- **Silent.** Nothing is written to the console or to storage.

### Out of scope

- A year in the date.
- A preview step.
- An "already shared" state.
- A payment receipt.
- Choosing a channel or sending from the app.
- Making the unit customizable (#318).
- Any other language (#317).

### Docs

`docs/product/sales.md` records the job, where Share lives, the bill text and its absent lines, the unit entry and the success criteria above. The glossary's **Sale bill** term gets its in-the-app wording ("Sale bill").

### Verdict

### Adjustments

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **true**. A new Share/Copy button on the sale's detail, a toast, a failure line and a selectable block.
- `touches_copy`: **true**. A new `saleBill` copy group: the button labels, the toast, two failure lines and the bill's fixed words and unit.

### PRD

#### Problem and job

When I have recorded a sale, or a customer later asks what they took or what they owe, I want to send them a short plain-text bill of that sale in one tap, so I can answer in our chat without retyping or working out figures, and the customer sees the same numbers I do.

The seller uses it at two moments:
- **Main:** right after saving, while the customer is still waiting in the chat.
- **Second:** later, when the customer asks. The seller opens that sale's detail and taps the same button.

Today the seller retypes the order and the total, or sends a screenshot, and works out the balance by hand. This is an assumption; a different answer would not change what is defined here. The story says "the chat" and names no channel (Assume nothing).

#### Scope

- On the sale's detail, a Share/Copy button sends that sale's **Sale bill**. The button is never inside the "Sale saved" notice.
- The bill is plain text and holds only facts. It is not an invoice; invoices stay out of scope in the brief.
- A payment receipt is not part of this job.
- Nothing about the bill is stored.

#### Success criteria

1. **One tap, no typing.**
   - On the phone, one tap either opens the phone's share sheet with the bill ready or puts the bill on the clipboard.
   - Choosing the chat and sending are the phone's own steps.
   - If sharing or copying fails, the seller still gets the text without typing.
2. **The figures match the app.**
   - The bill's total, payment state, still-owed amount and balance always agree with the sale's detail.
   - They come from the same settlement pass, in the same response as the detail's "still owing". Nothing on the front works settlement out again.
   - Evidence: a reference set of bills whose expected texts are written by hand against this PRD, never generated, with 0 mismatches. It covers these cases:
     - with and without a bulk line;
     - a VIP line and a special line, both reading like list lines;
     - a gift line;
     - a $0 sale of gifts only, with a balance that is not zero;
     - paid in cash, paid by transfer, paid later, partly owed, fully owed;
     - a balance above $0, a credit, and a balance of exactly $0;
     - no customer;
     - with and without shipping;
     - one line and several lines;
     - quantities 0,01 · 0,1 · 1,05 · 1.000.
3. **Nothing private leaks.**
   - The bill has no note, no customer name, no VIP, special or list wording, no ids and no links.
   - A VIP line or a special-price line reads like a list line.
   - Evidence: the leak test, which also checks that the text has no `*`, `_` or backtick.
4. **Assume nothing holds.**
   - Every fixed word and the unit come from the copy module.
   - The unit is one copy entry, "g" by default, until #318.
   - Evidence: with the unit set to "u", the bill reads "3,5 u" and "/u". A guard also checks that no sale bill source holds a "g" literal or calls the existing grams formatter.

#### The bill text

In this order:
1. **Header:** the business name, then on a new line the date as dd/mm, with no year. A blank line follows.
2. **One line per sale line:**
   - a list, VIP or special line: `{product} {quantity} {unit} · {amount}`
   - a bulk line: `{product} {quantity} {unit} · bulk price {unitPrice}/{unit} · {amount}`
   - a gift: `{product} {quantity} {unit} · Gift · $ 0`
3. **Shipping:** `Shipping · {amount}`, only when shipping charged is above $0.
4. **Total:** a blank line, then `Total · {amount}`.
5. **Payment line:**
   - "Paid in cash" or "Paid by transfer" for an on-the-spot payment;
   - "Paid" when the sale was paid in full later;
   - `Still owed on this sale: {amount}` when something is still owed on it, partly or fully;
   - no line when the payment state is none.
6. **Balance line:**
   - `Your balance: {amount}` when the balance is above $0;
   - `In your favor: {amount}` for a credit, shown as its magnitude.

**Formats:**
- Quantities are worked out from whole hundredths of a gram, never from a float. They use Argentine separators and drop trailing zeros: 0,01 · 0,1 · 1,05 · 3,5 · 1.000. The app's existing grams display reads exactly as it does today.
- Money reads `$ 11.000`, never in the catalog's format.

**Lines that can be missing:**
- Shipping, when it is $0.
- The payment line, when the payment state is none.
- The balance line, when the sale has no customer or the balance is exactly $0.

The balance is the customer's whole account at the moment of sharing, not on the sale's date. For a sale shared later the two can differ, and this is intended.

#### Button behaviour

- **Label:** Share where the phone can share; otherwise Copy.
- **Share:**
  - Success shows nothing; the share sheet closing is the confirmation.
  - Cancelling shows nothing.
  - Failure shows "Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste it into the chat." with the attention icon, and the label becomes Copy.
- **Copy:**
  - Success shows the toast "Bill copied".
  - Failure shows "Couldn't copy. Select the text below and copy it by hand." with the bill in a selectable block whose accessible name is "Sale bill".
- **No preview:** the text appears on screen only when copying fails.
- **No bill:**
  - The button is hidden when the server gives no bill. If the server can't build a bill, it gives none, and the rest of the sale's detail still shows.
  - A sale with no customer keeps the button, and its bill has no balance line.
- **Loading:** the button keeps its label and is disabled and busy. It shows no digits and no `$`.
- **Not found:** a missing sale takes the existing not-found path.
- **Fresh text:** the text is always built from the latest detail the screen holds. The detail refreshes as it does today.
- **Phone:**
  - The tap target is at least 44 px.
  - It works over the home network on plain http: the share or copy call runs right inside the tap, with nothing awaited before it (ADR-0002).
  - It follows the design tokens and the mobile check, with no new offender.
- **Silent:** nothing is written to the console or to storage.

#### Out of scope

- A year in the date.
- A preview step.
- An "already shared" state.
- A payment receipt.
- Choosing a channel, or sending from the app.
- Making the unit customizable (#318).
- Any other language (#317).

#### Docs

`docs/product/sales.md` records:
- the job;
- where Share lives;
- the bill text and the lines that can be missing;
- the unit entry;
- the success criteria and metrics above.

The glossary's **Sale bill** term gets its in-the-app wording.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:
- **Settled, do not reopen.** The request's technical plan stands as written: `formatQuantity`, the `saleBill` copy group with `en-XA` regenerated, the pure `renderSaleBill`, the hand-written `saleBill.cases.json`, the OpenAPI-snapshot key-list vitest, and the button's synchronous share or copy call. The seven invariants hold as written. This PRD adds only:
  - a reference case where the customer's balance is $0 (no balance line);
  - the reference cases listed under Success criteria.
- **Where the button sits on the sale's detail** is your call and the design's, under Drawing as guide. It must never sit inside the "Sale saved" notice.
- **WalkState** needs the copy-failed and loading states. The mobile e2e project must pass with no new offender.
- **A $0 sale of gifts only** shows whichever payment state the server already derives. The reference set records that state; nothing on the front invents one.
- **Docs:** I updated `docs/product/sales.md` and the **Sale bill** glossary term. Nothing else in the docs needs to change.

### Clarifications (round 2)

### Answers to the Tech Lead (round 2)

**Q1: `*`, `_` and backtick in names the seller typed.** Your default is correct. The rule covers only the bill's own fixed words. Names the seller typed go out exactly as typed, even when they contain these characters.

- **The bill never changes the seller's data.** The business name and every variety name are the seller's own data. The bill sends them exactly as stored: nothing escaped, removed or replaced. The customer reads the same name the seller sees in the app, which matches criterion 21: the customer sees the same things I do.
- **The no-`*`, `_` or backtick rule is about the bill's own words.** The bill adds no formatting of its own. No fixed word, separator or format in the copy module holds these characters. That is what keeps the bill plain text.
- **Escaping would break Assume nothing.** Which characters count as formatting depends on the chat app: Telegram, WhatsApp and others each have different rules, and some have none. Escaping for one app would build a channel into the bill, and the job deliberately names "the chat", never a channel. How a chat app shows a name is that app's business.
- **What this means for the tests:**
  - The leak test checks the fixed text. Its fixture names hold none of these characters, so any `*`, `_` or backtick it finds came from the bill itself.
  - The reference set gets one more case: a variety named with `_`, `*` and a backtick (e.g. ``OG*Kush_`x` ``), whose line shows the name unchanged.

I clarified `docs/product/sales.md` to match: the "Never in the bill" line, criterion 22, the Sale bill facts, the leak-test metric and the reference set.

## UX Designer

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

_Reaped 1 background process(es): 1 term_

### Flow

Evidence base: the PRD, and `VentaDetalle.dc.html` / `VentaDetalleCompu.dc.html`. The drawing shows the back "‹ Ventas", the title "Venta del 18/09", the total, the lines, shipping, total and "Borrar venta". It draws **no Share/Copy button, no "Sale saved" notice, no toast, no share-failed line and no selectable "Sale bill" block**. Where the button sits is not drawn. I have not invented a screen. UX Lead should flag the missing drawing, since the current screens cannot support this flow as drawn.

The design standards bear on the button. It is an action, so it is icon + text, and Share is the main action here, so it keeps its word. The icon is decorative. It is not drawn as a link. It is at least 44 px. Share's own icon-only exemption does not apply, because Share is this screen's main action.

### Job: send the customer the bill right after saving (main moment)

**Start:** the seller has just saved a sale. They land on `VentaDetalle` with the "Sale saved" notice (which has Undo). The customer is waiting in the chat.

**Steps, with the phone able to share (the usual case):**
1. `VentaDetalle` shows the detail. The Share button is below the notice, outside it. This is touch 1: tap Share.
2. The phone's share sheet opens with the bill text ready. This is the phone's own screen, not the app's.
3. The seller picks the chat and sends. Those steps belong to the phone, not the app.
4. The seller ends back on `VentaDetalle`, with nothing shown. A cancelled share also shows nothing.

**Steps, with the phone unable to share (plain http, or no share support):**
1. The button reads Copy. Touch 1: tap Copy.
2. The toast "Bill copied" appears.
3. The seller switches to the chat and pastes. That is the phone's own step.

**Touch count:** 1 touch in the app, for either label. Choosing the chat and sending are the phone's own steps. The seller types nothing.

**What can go wrong, and how they recover:**
- **Share fails.**
  - The attention icon and "Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste it into the chat." appear on `VentaDetalle`.
  - The label flips to Copy.
  - Recovery: tap Copy, which is 1 more touch (2 in total), then the toast "Bill copied". The seller then pastes in the chat.
- **Copy fails.**
  - "Couldn't copy. Select the text below and copy it by hand." appears, with the bill in a selectable block named "Sale bill".
  - Recovery: long-press, select and copy by hand in the phone's own steps. The seller still gets the text without typing.
  - The block's placement is not drawn.
- **Share is cancelled.** Nothing shows. The seller can tap again.
- **Loading.**
  - The button keeps its label and is disabled and busy. It shows no digits or `$`.
  - Recovery: wait for the detail to load, with no action needed.
- **No bill from the server.** The button is hidden. The rest of the detail still shows. There is no in-app recovery. This is not drawn and no message is specified.
- **Sale not found.** The existing not-found path applies, and the flow does not change.

**Where they end up:** on `VentaDetalle`, with the "Sale saved" notice and its Undo untouched. The button is never inside the notice.

### Job: send the bill later, when the customer asks

**Start:** the seller opens Sales, then the sale's row, which is `VentaDetalle` (row tap is touch 1 or more from a tab root: tab, then row).

**Steps:**
1. On Sales (`Ventas`), tap the sale's row. This is touch 1.
2. On `VentaDetalle`, tap Share or Copy. This is touch 2.
3. The outcomes and recovery paths are the same as in the first job. The balance line is the customer's whole account now, not on the sale's date, so it may differ from the first bill.

**Touch count:** 2 from the Sales list (row, then Share or Copy), or 3 from Home or another tab (tab, row, button). A failed share adds 1 (Copy).

**Notes:**
- A sale with no customer keeps the button, and its bill has no balance line.
- If the seller returns to the screen, the text reflects the latest detail (the detail refetches on focus).
- The back stays "‹ Ventas" and no second back is drawn. The Share button is no new route.

### Job: the sale's detail in its other uses (view, delete, undo)

**Flow unchanged.** The PRD adds only the button. Viewing the detail, "Borrar venta" with its confirmation, and Undo in "Sale saved" keep their steps and touch counts. The new button must not push "Borrar venta" or the notice out of reach on the phone. This is not drawn, so UX Lead should judge it.

### Job: computer layout

The flow is the same job in `VentaDetalleCompu`. There is no drawn button there either. The touch counts are the same, as clicks. The computer gets no dedicated polish (Phone first).

### Points for UX Lead (not resolved here)

1. Neither detail drawing has the Share/Copy button, the share-failed line, the copy-failed block, the toast or the loading state. The "Sale saved" notice is not drawn on `VentaDetalle` either.
2. The button's position relative to "Borrar venta" and the notice is undecided. The PRD leaves it to the design.
3. Share-failed has an attention icon in the PRD, and copy-failed has none. The listed notices in the standards do not name either of them. The "generic server-error retry" is the closest match.

### Walk

I walked the prototype from `Inicio.dc.html`.

**Job 1: send the bill right after saving (main moment)**

- **NuevaVenta, "Guardá la venta":** in the prototype this links to `Ventas.dc.html`, not to `VentaDetalle`. The flow follows `sales.md` (Saving: "the app opens the sale's detail … with the 'Sale saved' notice"), and the written definition wins over the drawing. So the flow's start is right, but the prototype cannot walk the main moment. The drawing needs fixing.
- **VentaDetalle, step 1 (Share/Copy): sin prototipo.**
  - The drawing has the back "‹ Ventas", "Venta del 18/09", the total in amber, the line, shipping, Total and "Borrar venta" at the bottom. It has no Share/Copy button.
  - "Sale saved" is a toast that lasts a few seconds (`sales.md`; drawn as a toast in `Estados` "Avisos al guardar"). "Below the notice" is therefore not a position: the button's place has to be set against the screen's own content.
- **Steps 2–4 (share sheet, pick the chat, send):** these are the phone's own screens, outside the prototype. On the text, the job takes 1 touch with no typing, which holds.
- **Copy path ("Bill copied"): sin prototipo.**
  - Right after saving, "Sale saved" with Undo may still be on screen when "Bill copied" arrives.
  - The flow says the notice and Undo stay "untouched" but not how the two toasts coexist. If one replaces the other, Undo goes away early. "Delete sale" still recovers, so this is friction, not a dead end.
- **Share failed: sin prototipo.** On the text, the label flips to Copy and 1 more touch reaches the clipboard. The recovery is complete.
- **Copy failed: sin prototipo.**
  - On the text, the selectable "Sale bill" block lets the seller copy by hand without typing. The recovery is complete.
  - The block's placement is not specified, nor whether it pushes "Borrar venta" off the phone screen.
  - Attention icon: the PRD gives it to share-failed and not to copy-failed, though the icon and text rule puts it on every notice that says something failed. Neither line is in the rule's list of six. This is for PM or UI Lead to settle.
- **Loading, cancel, not found:** on the text, nothing stops the job.
- **No bill (`bill: null`):** the button is hidden and the seller gets no explanation, so they fall back to retyping. The PRD settled this, so it is friction only.

**Job 2: send the bill later**

- I walked Inicio → Ventas tab → a sale's row → `VentaDetalle`. Every link exists, which confirms the flow's "3 from Home, 2 from Sales".
- The tap on the button itself is sin prototipo, as in Job 1. Its outcomes are the same as in Job 1.
- The back reads "‹ Ventas", and there is one back only. That is consistent with the flow.

**Job 3: the detail's other uses**

- "Borrar venta" is in the prototype, at the bottom above the tab bar. Undo lives in the toast.
- The flow leaves out "Assign customer", which `sales.md` puts on the detail. It also competes for space with the new button on the phone.

**Job 4: computer**

- `VentasCompu`'s rows link back to `VentasCompu`, not to `VentaDetalleCompu`, so the list → detail step cannot be walked.
- `VentaDetalleCompu` draws no button. Sin prototipo; the click counts rest on the text.

None of these steps stops the job: every failure the flow names has a recovery that gets the text to the chat without typing.

### Verdict

`approved`: both jobs can be completed and every failure has a recovery. The missing drawing, the button's position, how the two toasts interact and the copy-failed icon are friction, recorded as observations.

## UI Designer

_Reaped 1 background process(es): 1 term_

### Screens

Drawings read: `VentaDetalle.dc.html` and `VentaDetalleCompu.dc.html`. Neither draws the Share/Copy button, the toast, the share-failed line, the copy-failed block or the loading state. Each state below is assembled from components the product already has, and every point where that falls short is listed as a gap for UI Lead.

#### VentaDetalle (phone), built from `VentaDetalle.dc.html`

**Applies as is.** Back "‹ Ventas" (the shell's only back), the amber total card, "Líneas", the line rows with their rule chip, "Envío cobrado", "Total", "Borrar venta" and the bar with Ventas current. Nothing here is redrawn.

**What the PRD adds: one Share/Copy button.**
- **Placement.** It goes in the bottom action stack (the block that holds "Borrar venta", `margin-top: auto`, 24px side padding, 10px gap), as the first item, above "Borrar venta".
  - It sits in the screen's content, never inside the "Sale saved" toast.
  - The stack stays at the bottom, so the detail's content does not move.
- **Form.** It is an action, so it is icon + text. Share is this screen's main action, so it keeps its word.
  - It is a filled button, 48px high, full width, with the product's existing primary action style, like "Guardar la venta" in `NuevaVenta`. It is not drawn as a link: no underline, no link style.
  - It has a leading icon at 22px, stroke 1.6, `aria-hidden`. The Share state uses `compartir`; the Copy state uses `copiar`. Both are existing `NombreIcono` names.
  - The visible text is the word alone: "Share" or "Copy". The accessible name is that word.
  - It must be at least 44px. It uses tokens only, `user-select: none`, `touch-action: manipulation` and an `:active` state like the other primary buttons.
- **Why not icon-only.** The closed icon-only list does not apply to a screen's main action.
- **"Borrar venta"** keeps its own style, a text + icon quiet action, and stays below the button. The button must not push "Borrar venta" out of reach. If the extra 48px plus the 10px gap does not fit at 320px with text at 130%, the content scrolls above the stack; the button and "Borrar venta" stay reachable.

**States.**
- **Filled, can share.** Label "Share", enabled.
- **Filled, cannot share.** Label "Copy", enabled.
- **Loading.**
  - The button keeps its label ("Share" or "Copy") and is disabled with `aria-busy="true"`.
  - It uses the product's existing disabled style (the muted fill the other primary buttons use while saving).
  - It shows no digits and no `$`. The screen's own loading skeleton, shaped like the content, stands for the figures.
- **Copied.** The toast "Bill copied" uses the existing toast component, the one drawn in `Estados` "Avisos al guardar".
  - It carries no icon and no attention icon (toasts take none).
  - It has no action.
- **Share cancelled.** Nothing changes.
- **Share failed.**
  - A one-line notice sits directly above the button, inside the action stack: "Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste it into the chat."
  - It is built from the existing notice, with the attention icon decorative beside the sentence, in the same colour and wording as other failure notices.
  - The label flips to "Copy" with the `copiar` icon.
  - The line is 62 characters, within the 80 limit.
- **Copy failed.**
  - A notice above the button, in the same position: "Couldn't copy. Select the text below and copy it by hand." (56 characters).
  - Under it comes a selectable block named "Sale bill", holding the bill text.
  - The block is built from the existing card surface, the one the "Líneas" rows sit on: radius and padding from the tokens, body size. It is `user-select: text`, and it is the only place on the screen where the text shows.
  - It sits between the totals and the action stack, so "Borrar venta" and the button stay below it and the page scrolls.
- **No bill (`bill: null`).** The button is absent and the stack holds "Borrar venta" only. No message is drawn, because the PRD settled it.
- **Not found.** The existing not-found screen applies as is.
- **"Sale saved" state.** This is the toast with Undo. It stays as the product draws it, and the button is outside it.

**Gaps for UI Lead.**
1. No drawing exists for the button, the failure notices, the selectable block or the loading state. The design above is an assembly, not a copy.
2. Whether the failure notices carry the attention icon is unsettled in the written rule. The PRD gives it to share-failed and not to copy-failed. The rule's closed list of six notices names neither, and it says every notice that says something failed gets the icon. I drew the PRD's version: icon on share-failed, none on copy-failed. UI Lead or PM must settle it.
3. The toast "Bill copied" may land while the "Sale saved" toast with Undo is still on screen. The product's toast component does not say whether they stack or replace each other. I did not invent a rule.
4. The `copiar` and `compartir` icons are assumed to exist in `NombreIcono`, since Copy and Share appear in the rule. If either does not, it is a new name that this screen uses, and each new icon is judged by eye.
5. `NuevaVenta` "Guardá la venta" links to `Ventas.dc.html` in the drawing, not to this detail. `sales.md` wins, and the drawing is out of this task's scope.

#### VentaDetalleCompu (computer), built from `VentaDetalleCompu.dc.html`

**Applies as is** for everything already drawn. The button, its states, the toast and the two failure notices use the same components and the same states as the phone. The button sits in the same position, at the foot of the detail's column, above "Borrar venta", with the same icon + text form. The computer gets no dedicated polish.

Gap: the computer drawing also has none of these states. `VentasCompu` rows link to `VentasCompu` itself, so the list → detail step is not walkable in the prototype.

#### Toast "Bill copied" (shared component)

- **Built from** the existing toast in `Estados` "Avisos al guardar".
- **Text** "Bill copied", with no action and no icon.
- **States** shown once and then gone. It has no loading or error state of its own.

#### Selectable block "Sale bill" (shared component)

- **Built from** the card surface and body type already used on this screen.
- **Contents** the plain bill text with line breaks kept, in the order the PRD gives.
- **Accessible name** "Sale bill".
- **State** it appears only after a failed copy. It never shows in any other state and has no empty state.
- **Gap.** The product has no drawn text-block component for pre-formatted text. UI Lead should confirm that the card surface with `white-space: pre-wrap` is acceptable.
