What was verified:
- **formatQuantity** uses integer arithmetic, and every brief case passes. formatGrams has the form the brief set, and its old tests are untouched.
- **Copy group:** the `SALE_BILL_TEXT` texts match the brief. Share, Copy, Paid in cash and Paid by transfer are reused from their existing copy homes.

- **Reference set:** it has 12 cases and covers every case in the brief. I checked the arithmetic by hand.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/ventas/SaleBillButton.tsx` (new): the Share/Copy button.
  - It takes `state` (loading, or ready with a bill or null), `refreshing` and `onCopied`.
  - The label comes from `canShare()` and a local `shareFailed`.
  - The tap sets a pending ref and bumps a sequence ref. It renders the text and calls `shareText` or `copyText` synchronously, then awaits the outcome. Only the latest tap's outcome applies.
  - `visibilitychange` back to visible releases the pending flag.
  - A failed share or copy shows its line with the attention icon. A failed copy also shows the text in a `role="region"` block named "Sale bill", holding a `<pre>`.
- `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.tsx`:
  - The button is first in `venta-detalle__acciones`.
  - One `notice` state (`assigned | copied | null`) replaces `assigned`.
  - `refreshing` is true while the assign is pending, and after a successful PUT until the loaded detail has a customer.
  - The pending branch now renders a busy `.cuerpo` with an empty title row, `Loading` and the loading button.
  - The error, offline and gone branches are untouched.
- `web/src/components/Boton.tsx`: optional `ariaBusy`. `web/src/components/AvisoExito.tsx`: "Bill copied" added to `SuccessToast`.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: `.sale-bill__bloque` and `.sale-bill__texto`, and the button's `touch-action` rule.
- `web/src/mobile-standard/` (`states.ts`, `registry.ts`, `statesSales.ts`) and `web/e2e/mobile/walkScreen.ts`: the `held` and `failClipboard` fields, two state names and two states.
- `docs/product/sales.md`: placement, the attention icon on the copy-failed line, and the busy wait after an assign.

### Tests
- `SaleBillButton.test.tsx` (props seam, share, clipboard and `execCommand` stubbed at the browser API):
  - loading, refreshing and null bill;
  - Share with the exact text;
  - a cancelled share shows nothing and the label stays Share;
  - a failed share shows the line with the icon and the label becomes Copy;
  - `execCommand` runs synchronously within the click (`fireEvent`) and `onCopied` follows;
  - a failed copy shows the line and the block holding the text;
  - a double tap calls share once;
  - after `visibilitychange`, a new tap shares again and a late failure of the first share shows nothing.
- `VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx` (screen seam, `fetch` faked):
  - Share text comes from the detail's bill;
  - the Copy toast over http;
  - the button is first in the actions and not in the Sale saved notice;
  - a null bill hides it;
  - loading is busy with no digit or `$`;
  - a 404 shows no button;
  - after an assign, the copy happens only once the detail is back;
  - a stale focus refetch leaves the button busy until the customer arrives.

### Decisions and assumptions
- `Boton` had no way to set `aria-busy`, so it gained the optional `ariaBusy` prop.
- The assign wait ends during render, not in an effect, because the linter denies setState in an effect.
- `WalkState` gained optional `held` and `failClipboard` fields. `failClipboard` is a walk-page init script that is reset after the state.
- The copy-failed line carries the attention icon, per the Tech Lead's decision; the docs are corrected.
- Touch-action uses a `:first-child` selector on the actions row. A broader rule would flip other buttons that are listed touch-action offenders.

### For the Tech Lead
- **The delivery is not green. `web-e2e` is red.** The gate's full e2e timed out at 1500 s twice, with `phone standard` cells and `tapBudget.spec.ts` failing. I did not diagnose `tapBudget`; it may be load. A one-cell run of `sh scripts/e2e.sh --project=mobile -g "phone standard at 375x812, claro"` reported:
  - **Copy button touch-action:** `button:Copy` was a new `touch-action` offender on several screens. I added the CSS rule and did not re-run e2e afterwards.
  - **New walk state `VentaBillNoSePudoCopiar`:** it reports new `chrome-at-load`, `chrome-mid-scroll` and `text-size` offenders for the bottom bar. I did not fix or list them.
  - **Walk state design:** the registry already walks the sale route held and loaded, so `VentaBillCargando` (`held`) duplicates VentaDetalle's held walk under a new name. It likely adds offenders and could be dropped.
  - **Cascade:** `Entrar` came back `unreachable` and 183 diffs were reported in total. I suspect `addInitScript` or `held` leaks across states, but did not confirm it. First thing to try: remove the two new states and re-run to see whether the cascade disappears.
- **Design comparison:** `scripts/design/compare.sh` failed with "the sample app was not ready in 120 s". I did not look at VentaDetalle's comparison, and `screens.json` is untouched.
- **Weak test:** the stale-focus-refetch test in `VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx` may pass because react-query cancels the in-flight stale fetch when the assign invalidates, rather than because of the wait logic.
- The earlier findings about the renderer and data-flow subtasks are not mine and were not touched.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/ventas/SaleBillButton.tsx` (new): the Share/Copy button.
  - It takes `state` (loading, or ready with a bill or null), `refreshing` and `onCopied`.
  - The label comes from `canShare()` and a local `shareFailed`.
  - The tap sets a pending ref and bumps a sequence ref. It renders the text and calls `shareText` or `copyText` synchronously, then awaits the outcome. Only the latest tap's outcome applies.
  - `visibilitychange` back to visible releases the pending flag.
  - A failed share or copy shows its line with the attention icon. A failed copy also shows the text in a `role="region"` block named "Sale bill", holding a `<pre>`.
- `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.tsx`:
  - The button is first in `venta-detalle__acciones`.
  - One `notice` state (`assigned | copied | null`) replaces `assigned`.
  - `refreshing` is true while the assign is pending, and after a successful PUT until the loaded detail has a customer.
  - The pending branch now renders a busy `.cuerpo` with an empty title row, `Loading` and the loading button.
  - The error, offline and gone branches are untouched.
- `web/src/components/Boton.tsx`: optional `ariaBusy` and a placement-only `className`. `web/src/components/AvisoExito.tsx`: "Bill copied" added to `SuccessToast`.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: `.sale-bill__boton` (touch-action), `.sale-bill__bloque` and `.sale-bill__texto`.
- `docs/product/sales.md`: placement, the attention icon on the copy-failed line, the busy wait after an assign, and a corrected line on the walk states.
- On this return:
  - removed the two walk states and their fields and hooks;
  - the touch-action selector now targets the button's own class;
  - the assign-then-copy test reads the copied text.

### Tests
- `SaleBillButton.test.tsx` (props seam, share, clipboard and `execCommand` stubbed at the browser API):
  - loading, refreshing and null bill;
  - Share with the exact text;
  - a cancelled share shows nothing and the label stays Share;
  - a failed share shows the line with the icon and the label becomes Copy;
  - `execCommand` runs synchronously within the click (`fireEvent`) and `onCopied` follows;
  - a failed copy shows the line and the block holding the text;
  - a double tap calls share once;
  - after `visibilitychange`, a new tap shares again and a late failure of the first share shows nothing.
- `VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx` (screen seam, `fetch` faked):
  - Share text comes from the detail's bill;
  - the Copy toast over http;
  - the button is first in the actions and not in the Sale saved notice;
  - a null bill hides it;
  - loading is busy with no digit or `$`;
  - a 404 shows no button;
  - after an assign, the textarea value `copyText` hands to `execCommand` ends with `Your balance: $ 41.000` from the second GET;
  - a stale focus refetch leaves the button busy until the customer arrives.

### Decisions and assumptions
- **No WalkState for loading or copy-failed.** A new state on the sale's route inherits the route's existing offenders (bottom bar, text size, every button's touch-action) under a new screen name, and the offender lists may only grow for a trade or a new registry row (`gate.md`). The registry row for VentaDetalle already walks the screen loaded and held. Both states are covered by the front tests. This was found by a temporary one-state spec, since deleted, and recorded in `sales.md`.
- `Boton` gained a placement-only `className` so touch-action reaches the bill button alone, not a positional selector.
- The assign wait ends during render, not in an effect, because the linter denies setState in an effect.
- The copy-failed line carries the attention icon, per the Tech Lead's decision.

### For the Tech Lead
- **E2E:**
  - I ran `phone standard at 320x568, claro`, `320x568, oscuro` and `375x812, claro` alone, each in the foreground. All three pass (5.7 to 6.9 min each). Those are the cells the gate showed red before.
  - The earlier Entrar `unreachable` and the 183 diffs are gone. They came from the two walk states.
  - The gate's last full e2e run lists no failed test. Every test it printed passes, and it ends with "timed out after 1500 s" while the mobile cells were unfinished. A mobile cell takes 6 to 8 min alone and longer under the parallel load. I could not shorten the project: that is the config's cost, not this subtask's diff.
  - The earlier renderer subtask review also recorded a 1500 s timeout with no failing test.
- **Design comparison:** `scripts/design/compare.sh` still stops with "the sample app was not ready in 120 s", so VentaDetalle's comparison was not looked at. `screens.json` is untouched.
- **Observation not acted on:** the stale-refetch test proves the busy wait between the PUT and the fresh GET, but react-query likely cancels the stale fetch, so the stale-data path itself is not exercised.
- The open findings about sister subtasks are not mine and were not touched.

### Changes
- `web/src/components/Boton.tsx`: the optional `className` prop is removed, so the component is back to its header rule. Only `ariaBusy` remains from this subtask.
- `web/src/screens/ventas/SaleBillButton.tsx`: the fragment is now `<div className="sale-bill">`, and the `className` on the button is gone.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: `.sale-bill { display: contents }` and `.sale-bill > button { touch-action: manipulation }` replace `.sale-bill__boton`.
- `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx`: the placement test finds the actions row with `closest` and expects the wrapper first in it.
- Unchanged this round: the pending ref and sequence, the notice slot, the loading skeleton, the docs, and the walk files (still at the task base).

### Tests
- `VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx` (screen seam, `fetch` faked) and `SaleBillButton.test.tsx` (component seam): 18 tests pass on this head.
- No new test: the change only moves where one CSS rule attaches, and the placement test covers the wrapper.
- Typecheck and lint pass.
- The gate's failures are in `tests/test_e2e_clock.py`, `src/screens/Variedades.test.tsx` (last gate run) and the e2e web server start. None of those files is in this diff.

### Decisions and assumptions
- Touch-action cannot go on the base `.boton` class: it would turn the listed touch-action offenders green, and the lists fail on an entry whose cell now passes. A wrapper scoped to the bill button is the narrowest rule that adds no offender.
- `display: contents` keeps the wrapper out of the layout, so the actions column and its 10 px gap are unchanged.
- The WalkState conflict (brief versus gate.md) stays as recorded in the previous delivery and needs a plan decision.

### For the Tech Lead
- **Look first:** `Boton.tsx` (only `ariaBusy` remains from this subtask) and the `.sale-bill` rules in `app.css`.
- **Gate:** the failures above are in untouched files. The machine's load average was 132 when I checked, and the clock check misses by seconds and by a minute boundary under load. I did not change the checks.
- **Not verified this round:** I did not re-run the mobile e2e after the CSS change, and `scripts/design/compare.sh` was not run again after it stopped on every earlier try. The three mobile cells I ran last round passed with the old selector, which matched the same button.
- **Rows I did not touch:** `saleBillContract.test.ts`, `CONTEXT.md`, the missing `WRITING_STANDARDS.md` and the flaky `CobroTree` test belong to other subtasks or to no subtask. The copy-failed icon is for the PM or UI Lead.

## Tech Lead: review sale-bill-button (v1)

### Verdict

**returned** (`process-suite-red`): The mobile e2e project is red on the new walk states (the brief requires it to pass with no new offender), and the assign-then-copy test never asserts the copied text the brief names. Fix the e2e first.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| blocking | process-suite-red | `web/src/mobile-standard/statesSales.ts:78` | The mobile e2e project is red. The new walk states add offenders (chrome-at-load, chrome-mid-scroll, text-size), Entrar comes back unreachable, and the phone standard cells fail. | `### Checks`, web-e2e full: **failed**. `✘ [mobile] mobile.spec.ts:38 phone standard at 320x568 oscuro / claro, 375x812 claro`, then `(timed out after 1500 s)`. The brief says: "passes with no new offender". |
| blocking | spec-missing-test | `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx:155` | The "copied text ends with the balance of the second GET" test never reads the copied text. | I ran `npm --prefix web test …SaleBillButton.test.tsx …VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx`: 18 passed. The test only asserts `execCommand` was called once (line 166) and the toast. `execCommand` is a bare `vi.fn`. The brief asks for a test that the copied text ends with `Your balance: $ …` from the second GET. |
| important | standard-other | `web/src/styles/app.css:2027` | The `.boton:first-child` selector is positional. When the bill is null it gives touch-action to Assign customer or Delete sale, and both are listed offenders, so their entries can fail as "now passes". | `SaleBillButton.tsx:52` returns null when the bill is null. `offenders/touch-action.json` lists those buttons. §10: the run fails "on an entry whose cell now passes". |
| observation | standard-other | `web/e2e/mobile/walkScreen.ts:71` | Only the clipboard is stubbed. Where Chromium exposes `navigator.share`, the label reads Share and both new states become unreachable. | |
| observation | spec-missing-test | `web/src/screens/ventas/VentaDetalle.bill.test.tsx:170` | The stale-refetch test proves the busy wait between the PUT and the fresh GET. react-query likely cancels the stale fetch, so the stale-data path itself is not exercised. | |
| observation | spec-scope | `scripts/design/screens.json` | VentaDetalle's design comparison was not looked at, because compare.sh did not start. | |

I verified the invariants on the diff. All twelve hold, with no break found:
- **1 and 2:** the bill comes from the last GET only. The assign invalidates the detail, with no `setQueryData`.
- **4:** every word comes from `SALE_BILL_TEXT`.
- **9:** `shareText`/`copyText` are called synchronously, with no await before them, and nothing is logged or stored.
- **10:** one pending ref plus a sequence number.
- **11:** loading is an explicit `{kind:'loading'}`.
- **12:** the error, offline and Gone branches are untouched.

The open rows in `## Tech Lead: findings` belong to sister subtasks, and this branch does not repeat them.

### For the Developer

1. **Make the `mobile` project of `npm --prefix web run e2e` pass with no new offender.**

### Review

I read `git diff fbc4fdc0...HEAD` and looked for every new or changed text a person reads. That covers the source catalog `web/src/copy/sales.ts` (`SALE_BILL_TEXT`), the regenerated `en.baseline.json`, the places that use the texts (`SaleBillButton.tsx`, `VentaDetalle.tsx`, `AvisoExito.tsx`, `saleBill.ts`), the glossary entry in `CONTEXT.md`, and `docs/product/sales.md`.

**Catalog texts.** All 19 `SALE_BILL_TEXT` entries match what the Tech Writer wrote, word for word:
- the labels and the toast;
- both failure lines and the block's accessible name;
- the header, item, bulk and gift patterns, with the " · " separator;
- shipping, total, the three paid states, still owed, balance and credit;
- the unit "g".

Four entries reuse texts already in the catalog: `share` and `copy` from `COMMON_TEXT`, and `paidCash` and `paidTransfer` from `CUSTOMERS_TEXT`. Their values are exactly "Share", "Copy", "Paid in cash" and "Paid by transfer", so nothing changes for the person reading them. Both failure lines are registered as `notice` in `LIMITED_COPY`, under 80 characters. No fixed word holds `*`, `_` or a backtick. The renderer and the button use no literal words. The en baseline matches the texts. No text was added that the Tech Writer did not write.

**Where the texts are used.**
- The toast "Bill copied" goes through the shared `AvisoExito` slot.
- "Sale bill" is the `aria-label` of the selectable region and is not a visible heading.

**Findings: none important, three observations.**
- **Attention icon on the copy-failed line.** The code shows it, and `sales.md` now documents it. The PRD gives the icon to the share-failed line only, and the Tech Writer left this open for the PM or UI Lead. The wording is fine either way. Someone needs to confirm the icon so the PRD and `sales.md` agree.

- **Copy path (the phone can't share, or plain http):** the label is Copy, with `copiar`. `copyText` runs inside the tap, and success shows "Bill copied" in the notice slot. - **Share failed:** "Couldn't share. Tap Copy and paste it into the chat." shows with the attention icon, and the label flips to Copy. One more tap reaches the clipboard. ✔

- **Loading:** the pending branch draws the action row with the button disabled, `aria-busy="true"`, keeping its label and showing no figures. After "Assign customer", the button stays busy until the detail with the customer arrives. ✔
- **No bill:** the button renders nothing, and the rest of the detail shows. **Not found:** the existing GoneScreen branch is unchanged. ✔

- Sales → a sale's row → `VentaDetalle` is unchanged, and so is the back. The same button, with the same outcomes. A focus refetch keeps the text built from the latest detail. 2 touches from Sales, 3 from another tab. ✔

**Job 3: the detail's other uses**
- The stack is a column: Share/Copy, then "Assign customer" (when there is no customer), then Delete (icon-only). Delete and its confirmation and Undo keep their steps. Delete stays reachable below the button.
- The button is drawn as the `secondary` Boton, not the filled primary the validated screen named. It is icon + text, at least 44 px and not a link, so the job holds. This is for UI Lead.

- Same component and same position. Judged from the code only.

- The last commit removed the loading and copy-failed `WalkState` entries the plan asked for, and `statesSales.ts` has no sale bill state. The phone walk covers neither state. The 320 px / 130 % scroll of the copy-failed block rests on the design capture.

## UI Lead: final comparisons (v1)

_The system denied 4 write attempts: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

_Reaped 1 background process(es): 1 term_

### Comparisons

**I could not judge any comparison by eye.** I ran the declared check (`scripts/design/compare.sh --only VentaDetalle,VentaDetalleCompu,VentaDetalleOscuro,VentaDetalleCompuOscuro`) twice. Both runs stopped with "the sample app was not ready in 120 s" while the shared machine was at a load average of about 88. This role cannot write anything, so I could not give the run a longer wait. I found older comparison sheets under `/tmp`, but they are not from this head: none of them draws the Share/Copy button. They show the detail as it was before this task. So what follows comes from reading the head's code (`VentaDetalle.tsx`, `SaleBillButton.tsx`, `app.css`) against the validated screens design, not from a capture.

**VentaDetalle (phone): parts already drawn.** The older sheets and the diff agree. The back, title, total card, lines with their chips, shipping, total and bar are untouched by this task. The built screen already differed from the drawing before this change: the back reads "‹ Sales", the total is not amber, and delete is an icon-only button rather than "Borrar venta" at the bottom. None of that comes from this task.

**Share / Copy button: Filled, can share / cannot share.** It is the first item in `venta-detalle__acciones`, before "Assign customer" and delete, and outside the toast. It has icon and text: `compartir` + "Share", or `copiar` + "Copy". Its tap target is a `Boton` (at least 44 px), with `touch-action: manipulation` and tokens only. It differs from the design in one way: it uses `variant="secondary"`, where the design asked for the filled primary style. It is still an action, not a link, and still reads as the same design.

**Loading.** This state shipped. The pending branch renders the title row, the skeleton, and the button in the action row, disabled and `aria-busy`, with no digits and no `$`. The same busy state also covers the refetch after an assign.

**Copied.** This state shipped. "Bill copied" uses the existing `AvisoExito` and shares one slot and timer with "Customer assigned". Its relation to the "Sale saved" toast is still the open observation from design time.

**Share cancelled / No bill / Not found.** Cancelling shows nothing. With `bill: null` the button is not rendered. The 404, offline and error branches are unchanged. All three shipped.

**Share failed.** This state shipped. An `AvisoAmbar` with the attention icon shows the share-failed line, and the label flips to Copy. It sits **below** the button, where the design put it directly above.

**Copy failed.** This state shipped. The copy-failed line has the attention icon (a Tech Lead decision, now recorded in `sales.md`). Directly beneath it is the `region` named "Sale bill", a `<pre>` with `pre-wrap` and `user-select: text` on the card surface with token radius and padding. The notice is below the button rather than above, and the block now sits right under the notice. That fixes the earlier "the text below points the wrong way" observation.

**VentaDetalleCompu, both themes.** The computer layout and both themes come from the same component and CSS, all on tokens. I did not capture or judge them by eye.

**320 px / 130 % text.** Not checked: there is no capture.

### Verdict

`approved`, on reading the code: every state the PRD requires shipped and is built from existing components. The differences I found still read as the same design. The by-eye comparison was not done because the capture could not run under the machine's load, so it should be attached before close.

### Comparisons

I ran the declared check, `scripts/design/compare.sh --only VentaDetalle,VentaDetalleCompu,VentaDetalleOscuro,VentaDetalleCompuOscuro`, on this head. The sample app came up this time, and the run produced design captures for all four rows. **No row produced a built capture.** I ran `VentaDetalle` and `VentaDetalleCompu` a second time and got the same result, so it is not a one-off.

**VentaDetalle (phone, light) and VentaDetalleOscuro (phone, dark)**
- The design side was captured as drawn: "‹ Ventas", "Venta del 18/09", the amber total, Líneas, Envío cobrado, Total, "Borrar venta" and the bar.
- The built side is `null`, with the note `shot: the page changed when the viewport grew (built)`. The sheet shows "—" where the app should be.
- An earlier run from before this task (`/tmp/tmp.NHZR7B7lKs`) did capture the built side of the same row with the same route, `/sales/{owing_sale}`. That capture shows the detail with no Share button. So the failure started with this change, most likely the new action row, the busy or skeleton logic, or the notice slot. I am inferring that cause; I did not test it.

**VentaDetalleCompu (computer, light) and VentaDetalleCompuOscuro (computer, dark)**
- The design side was captured.
- The built side is `null`, with the note `shot: page taller than its viewport (built)`. Again, nothing built can be judged.

**What could not be judged by eye**
- The Share/Copy button in the filled state: where it sits, its icon + text form, and how it reads next to delete.
- The loading state, the toast "Bill copied", share-failed, copy-failed with the "Sale bill" block, and the no-bill state.
- 320 px with text at 130 %.

The previous UI Lead's code reading found that every state the PRD requires is implemented. They also noted three small differences: a secondary rather than filled style, the failure notices below the button rather than above it, and the copy-failed block now placed right under its notice. None of these can be confirmed visually while the built capture fails.

Under the design-capture standard, a row that cannot capture its screen fails under `--strict` and is a defect to fix, never a known failure. This head is the first to fail on these rows. So there is no evidence that the built screen keeps equal-to-the-eye fidelity to the design, in any viewport or theme.

### Verdict

`returned`: on this head, none of the four VentaDetalle rows produces a built capture (in two runs). The rows captured before this change, so the new Share/Copy screen and its states cannot be judged by eye until the capture is fixed and rerun.

### Integration

Merged: all three branches were already in the task branch when I started (`git merge-base --is-ancestor` is true for each): `agentic/GH-376.sale-detail-bill-data`, `agentic/GH-376.sale-bill-renderer` and `agentic/GH-376.sale-bill-button`. Nothing was left to merge, and there were no conflicts.

Checks on the merged branch:
- `types` (mypy) and `lint` (ruff): clean.
- `web-types` and `web-lint`: pass, before and after my commit.
- Web vitest subset (`saleBillContract`, `src/components`, `src/screens/ventas`, `src/design`, `src/styles`): 660 of 661 pass. The one failure is `web/src/design/census.test.ts:163`, the Reset design's network-settle timeout. It was already flagged as flaky under load and this diff does not touch it.
- `tests` (pytest -n auto), delivery gate: 3481 passed and 1 failed, `tests/test_e2e_clock.py::test_module_execution_prints_only_the_offset_integer`. It asserts a clock offset within 5 s and measured 7 s. That is a wall-clock timing check, the run took 15 minutes under heavy load, and this front-only diff changes nothing under `app/`. I treat it as load-induced. The process I backgrounded earlier finished with exit code 0.
- `web-e2e`, delivery gate: it timed out at 1500 s, and `e2e/payments.spec.ts` showed ✘ after 2.9 min. Run alone afterwards, that spec passes in 24 s. I could not run the full e2e or the `mobile` project to completion myself, because a foreground run is capped at 600 s. The `.boton` change is therefore not confirmed by the mobile project.

Fixes I made for findings left open by the reviews (commit 5d7ef989):
- **`Boton` free `className` prop** (Boton.tsx:52). I removed the prop and the `sale-bill__boton` class. The `touch-action: manipulation` rule now sits in the shared `.boton` base in `web/src/styles/app.css`, so the Boton header's rule of no one-off class is true again. `SaleBillButton` no longer passes `className`. `ariaBusy` stays.
- **Drift-rejection test** (`web/src/api/saleBillContract.test.ts`). The three contract assertions and the two enum assertions now go through the same `sameSet` helper that the rejection test uses. The rejection test feeds it drifted lists (an extra `note` key, a dropped key, an extra `refunded` state), so the guard is proven against the comparison that guards the contract. The docstring is now true.

Plan invariants 1 to 12 were not touched by the merge, and I changed nothing for them. The Tech Lead verified them on the integrated diff.

- **Dropped WalkState entries, needs a plan decision.** The brief asks for `loading` and `copy-failed` WalkState entries. They were added and then dropped in a60d1d80, and `docs/product/sales.md` was rewritten to say none are added, because §10's offender lists cannot take a new screen name's cells. I did not decide this; either the plan or §10 has to give.
- **Copy-failed line's attention icon.** The line carries the icon (`AvisoAmbar attention`) and the docs say so. The PRD gives the icon only to share-failed, and the Tech Writer left it open for the PM or UI Lead.
- **`WRITING_STANDARDS.md`** is still missing from the product, so these texts have no criteria.
- **`CONTEXT.md:509`:** the capital letter after the semicolon in "; In the app:" is untouched.
- **Check first:** the `.boton` `touch-action` move touches a shared style. The e2e is the place to see any visual or offender effect, and the suite as a whole is slow enough to time out here.

### Integration

Merged: all three branches (`agentic/GH-376.sale-detail-bill-data`, `agentic/GH-376.sale-bill-renderer` and `agentic/GH-376.sale-bill-button`) were already in the task branch, so there was nothing to merge and there were no conflicts. My commits on top of them:

- **601eb050:** an empty commit, there only because a delivery needs a commit. It records the finding below.

This return (PM final validation, the VentaDetalle design capture): **no code changed.**

**What I measured**
- `scripts/design/compare.sh --strict --only VentaDetalle,VentaDetalleCompu` on this head gives:
  - Phone: `shot: the page changed when the viewport grew (built)`.
  - Computer: `shot: page taller than its viewport (built)`.
- With `--census`, the built document is 954 px at a 390x844 viewport, and 951 px at 1280x920. The design side is 980 px and 920 px.
- I started the sample app by hand and probed the page.
  - Phone structure: `div.flecha-atras` (31 px), `main.pantalla` (844 px, from `.pantalla { min-height: 100dvh }`), then `nav.bar-inferior` (79 px), so the document is 954 px.
  - Growing the viewport to 954 px makes `main` 954 px tall and the document 1064 px, so the page never fits its viewport.
  - The bottom bar's top moves from 875 to 985 px. That is the "changed when the viewport grew" miss. The computer page is the same, with a 31 px arrow over a `main` of 920 px.

**Why I stopped.** The same two failures, with identical numbers (954 and 951), appear when I check out all of `web/src` at the task's base `937fc35d~1` and run the same compare. I restored `web/src` from HEAD afterwards, and `git status` is clean. So the failure is not caused by this branch's screen code (`VentaDetalle.tsx`, `SaleBillButton.tsx`, `.sale-bill` styles) or by the new button. It comes from the shell's layout for cards with a back arrow, which this branch does not touch. I could not reproduce the Tech Lead's claim that these rows captured before this change, and I did not find another cause in the time left (the turn cap was close).

**What I did not do.** I did not edit `compare.sh`, `screens.json`, the shell or the `.pantalla` rules. I did not drop, relax or mark any of the four rows. A change to shell layout would affect every card with a back arrow, which is outside this task's diff.

- `src/screens/ventas` passes (14 files, 116 tests).

The earlier gate runs on this head failed different sets (5, 6 and 3 tests), and each spec I re-ran alone passed, so I read them as load flakes.

### Integration

Merge: nothing was left to merge. The three subtask branches (`sale-detail-bill-data`, `sale-bill-renderer`, `sale-bill-button`) were already in the branch, with no conflicts. Earlier rounds changed these:
- `Boton`'s free `className` prop was removed (5d7ef989).
- `touch-action: manipulation` is scoped to `.sale-bill > .boton` under a wrapper div in `SaleBillButton` (d8988ad8).
- The contract test's drift check uses the one `sameSet` that the real assertions use.

This round: the capture failure on the four VentaDetalle rows.
- **Cause.** The shell is `.columna-principal` > `.flecha-atras` (31 px) + `main.pantalla` + `nav.bar-inferior` (79 px). `.pantalla` has `min-height: 100dvh`. Inside the shell the document was therefore the arrow, a full viewport and the bar, about 110 px taller than the viewport. Growing the viewport re-grew `main`, so the bar moved (phone: "the page changed when the viewport grew") and the page never fit (computer: "page taller than its viewport").
- **Not specific to the sale detail.** ClienteFicha and RetiroDetalle failed the same way. ClienteEditarCompu and EntradaStockEditar captured fine.
- **Fix (76e0c892).** `.disposicion .pantalla` now has `min-height: 0; flex: 1`. `.disposicion` already holds a viewport, so the screen takes what the arrow and the bar leave. No check in the capture tooling was loosened, and `screens.json` is unchanged.
- **Result.** `comparisons.mjs --strict --only VentaDetalle,VentaDetalleOscuro,VentaDetalleCompu,VentaDetalleCompuOscuro` gave `ok` on all four rows, exit 0. The built PNGs for VentaDetalle (phone) and VentaDetalleCompuOscuro (computer, dark) show the Copy button with its icon first in `venta-detalle__acciones` (the delete button follows), and no "Sale saved" notice. I did not open the other two PNGs.
- **How I ran it.** `compare.sh` itself stopped at its 120 s sample-app wait under machine load. I started the sample app by hand and ran `comparisons.mjs --strict --only …` against it, which is the command `compare.sh` runs.

Not verified after the fix:
- **Mobile phone-standard e2e.** My own run of `-g "320x568, claro"` was cut off by my timeout, so I did not rerun it.
- **Gate runs on 76e0c892.** In the first run three mobile cells failed (320x568 claro, 375x812 claro, 320x568 oscuro) after about 4 minutes each, and the run timed out at 1500 s. The same run's pytest failure was `tests/test_e2e_clock.py::test_module_execution_prints_only_the_offset_integer`, offset 10 s against 5 s, a wall-clock test under a load average of about 35. In the second run every test the gate showed as finished passed, but the run timed out at 1500 s with the mobile cells still running.
- **Previous head.** d8988ad8 passed the mobile cells I ran at 320x568 and 246x437, claro.
- **Still open.** I cannot tell from here whether the mobile failures are load or this commit.

### For the Tech Lead

- **Mobile standard first.** The shell rule changes layout for every card with a back arrow and the bar. If the mobile cells fail with `fixedButListed` or `unlisted` entries, that is this commit. Cards whose content is shorter than a viewport now fit exactly one viewport. Their screens used to overflow by about 110 px, so listed offenders about overflow or scroll may now pass, and the standard would then ask to delist them. If instead the cells time out near 4 minutes, that looks like load.
- **Shell-wide change.** The rule is at `web/src/styles/app.css`, in `.disposicion .pantalla`. An alternative is a narrower selector, but the other two failing rows (ClienteFicha, RetiroDetalle) have the same cause.
- **Still open and not mine to decide:**
  - The `loading` and `copy-failed` WalkState entries were dropped from `statesSales.ts`, which conflicts with the §10 offender rule.
  - The copy-failed line carries the attention icon.
  - `docs/product/sales.md:409` has scope wording to check.
  - `CONTEXT.md:509` has a capital letter after a semicolon.
  - `WRITING_STANDARDS.md` is missing.

`affected-only` rows ran the tests selected against `fbc4fdc0` (the whole branch, `task_base..head`, not the increment under review); the full suite runs at merge (ADR-0045).

`web-e2e`, last lines:

```text
affected: unsound -> full
```

### Integration

Merge: nothing was left to merge. The three subtask branches (`sale-detail-bill-data`, `sale-bill-renderer`, `sale-bill-button`) were already in the branch, with no conflicts. Earlier rounds changed these:
- `Boton`'s free `className` prop was removed (5d7ef989).
- `touch-action: manipulation` is scoped to `.sale-bill > .boton` under a wrapper div in `SaleBillButton` (d8988ad8).
- The contract test's drift check uses the one `sameSet` that the real assertions use.

Capture fix (76e0c892):
- **Cause.** The shell is `.columna-principal` > `.flecha-atras` (31 px) + `main.pantalla` + `nav.bar-inferior` (79 px). `.pantalla` has `min-height: 100dvh`. Inside the shell the document was the arrow, a full viewport and the bar, about 110 px taller than the viewport. Growing the viewport re-grew `main`, so the bar moved (phone) and the page never fit (computer).
- **Fix.** `.disposicion .pantalla` now has `min-height: 0; flex: 1`, so the screen takes what the arrow and the bar leave. ClienteFicha and RetiroDetalle failed to capture the same way. No check in `compare.sh` or the capture tooling was loosened, and `screens.json` is unchanged: none of the four rows is dropped, made non-strict or marked as a known failure.
- **Result on this head.** `scripts/design/compare.sh --strict --only VentaDetalle,VentaDetalleOscuro,VentaDetalleCompu,VentaDetalleCompuOscuro` printed `ok` for all four, exit 0. I opened all four built PNGs. Each shows the Copy button with its icon first in `venta-detalle__acciones`, above the delete button, with no "Sale saved" notice.

Offender lists (cfdb31ab), the Tech Lead's blocking finding:
- The shell rule made listed offenders pass, so the phone-standard e2e failed on `fixedButListed`.
- I ran the four claro and four oscuro cells on 76e0c892 (`--project=mobile -g "phone standard at .*, claro"` and `.*, oscuro`). Every failure was `fixedButListed` with `unlisted: []`. They freed 162 cells, all issue 59, across 18 screens in `chrome-at-load` and 15 in `chrome-mid-scroll`. All but a few are `navigation:Navigation`, and some are `link:New sale`.
- Examples: Archivadas, ClientesBusqueda, Configuracion, EstadosGone, EstadosReintento, Mas, RetiroDetalle, TodasLasEntradasVariedad, VentaDetalle, VerTodas.
- I removed exactly those cells from `chrome-at-load.json` (61 entries to 57) and `chrome-mid-scroll.json` (56 to 53). I added no entries and touched no other offender file.
- Result: `src/mobile-standard/offenders` vitest, 25 passed. Both mobile runs on cfdb31ab (claro and oscuro, `--workers=4`) ended `passed` with exit 0. That covers all eight phone-standard cells plus the setup, with `unlisted` and `fixedButListed` empty.

Not run after the last commit: the full web-e2e (it takes about 22 minutes and timed out at 1500 s on earlier gate runs), pytest, tsc, lint and format. The commit changes only two JSON offender files. On earlier gate runs the pytest failure (`test_e2e_clock.py`, offset 10 s against 5 s) and the e2e failures were different each time and passed alone.

### For the Tech Lead

- **Shell-wide change.** The rule in `web/src/styles/app.css` affects every card with a back arrow and the bar, and 162 delisted cells show how far it reaches. The diff to review is the two offender JSON files: confirm that no entry for a screen unrelated to this layout was removed. The removed list is exactly what the e2e reported as fixed, so it is derived from the run, not from me.
- **Captures.** The built PNGs are in the temporary directory of the last compare run, `/tmp/tmp.9uTJyaFnm6/comparisons/`. They are not committed and I could not attach them to the PR.
- **Still open and not mine to decide:**
  - The `loading` and `copy-failed` WalkState entries were dropped from `statesSales.ts` (§10 conflict; the plan needs to decide).
  - The copy-failed line carries the attention icon, while the PRD gives it only to share-failed.
  - `docs/product/sales.md:409` states no `WalkState` is added for copy-failed or loading, which disagrees with the request.
  - `CONTEXT.md:509` has a capital letter after a semicolon.
  - `WRITING_STANDARDS.md` is missing.

### Why it does not land

The branch has never taken in master. `origin/agentic/GH-376` (head `7a00e04b`) has no merge commit. Its merge base with `origin/master` is still `fbc4fdc0`, "Merge pull request #421 … GH-375", from 25 Oct. Master (`ae408a8b`) is 77 commits ahead.

Those 77 commits change exactly what keeps this PR red:
- `294e9a2b` "Gate merges on GitHub's test and web jobs" (`.agentic/stack.yaml`).
- `8bbc1db2` and `b9f04c4b`: the census test now runs as its own vitest project, because the failure was starvation under load.
- `7a74ad9b` and `d349c445`: the census fixture no longer depends on when the resize event arrives.
- `6bc9ed81`: xdist is capped at 8 workers.
- `011398cc` and `87b1b098`: e2e stays out of the gated checks.

This branch's reds match those fixes:
- The gate's web-tests failure is `web/src/design/census.test.ts:156`, which "passes alone on this head".
- `CobroTree.test.tsx:92` is "flaky under load".
- The repeated "web-e2e timed out at 1500 s with no failing test" is the local e2e that master has turned off.

None of these reds is on a file the branch changes. The work itself is approved:
- The Tech Lead approved `24ebec1a`.
- The four blocking capture findings were fixed in the shell (`76e0c892`, `web/src/styles/app.css`, 6 lines). Their evidence is in `7a00e04b`, `docs/evidence/GH-376/after/VentaDetalle*.png`.

I compared the files changed on each side. No file is changed by both. The only master commits in the areas this branch touches are `118059fa` and `8699d626`, which change `web/src/copy/limits.ts` and `copyLimits.test.ts`; the branch changes neither. Both changes are backward-compatible for this branch:
- `LimitedCopy` became a union that still accepts `{ kind, text }`, which is what `web/src/copy/sales.ts:54` uses.
- The new `NAMESPACE` path applies only to keyed modules, and `sales.ts` is not one.
- Master's census change touches only synthetic fixture pages, not `app.css`.

What I could not read: GitHub's actual check results and the merge-refusal message. `gh` and `git merge-tree` were denied in this role. So "the reds are the census/load failures master fixed" comes from the findings and the history, not from the CI logs themselves.

### What a fresh run must do differently

- Do not rebuild anything. Merge current `origin/master` into `agentic/GH-376` and push. Then let GitHub's `test` and `web` jobs, now the merge gate, run on the merged head.
- Expect no textual conflicts. After the merge, run `tsc` strict and the copy-limits vitest (`web/src/copy/copyLimits.test.ts`) once. They confirm that `sales.ts`'s `LIMITED_COPY` still type-checks against master's new `LimitedCopy` union and the new 3-argument `copyProblems`.
- Do not re-run or re-record the full local web-e2e. Master has turned it off locally (`011398cc`), and its 1500 s timeouts were never this diff's.
- One plan finding is still open, and it is not why the merge is refused: the `loading` and `copy-failed` `WalkState` entries were dropped (`web/src/mobile-standard/statesSales.ts:75`, `docs/product/sales.md:409`) because §10's offender lists cannot take them. It needs a plan decision, not more code on this branch. Do not quietly re-add the states, and do not grow the offender lists to make room for them.

### Why it does not land

The cause is not the code and not a conflict. The task record carries six **blocking**, plan-scope findings whose location is `'PM: final validation'`. Each one is still `status: open` (`.agentic/tasks/GH-376.md:1031`, `:1041`, `:1051`, `:1084` and the two after them).

Those findings are the capture criteria from PM's v2 and v3 returns: all four VentaDetalle rows capture, nothing is loosened in `compare.sh` or `screens.json`, and the captures are attached. The branch meets all of them:
- The shell fix is in `76e0c892` (`web/src/styles/app.css:443-450`).
- The offender lists were shrunk to match in `cfdb31ab`.
- The four captures are committed in `docs/evidence/GH-376/after/` (`7a00e04b`).
- PM's final validation has said `approved` from v4 onward, and the integration subtask is `status: approved`.

Still, `settled_findings` (`:1284-1299`) holds only file locations. None of them is `PM: final validation`, so no review ever closes these six. The last three landing refusals (`return_history`, `:154`, `:188`, `:222`, all `by: landing`) quote exactly these six claims. The integration subtask's `reason` repeats them on `reviewed_head: 74d56a3f` (`:571`).

That head is already the sync. `refs/heads/agentic/GH-376` shows the merge of master `ae408a8b` ("Merge made by the 'ort' strategy"), pushed to `origin/agentic/GH-376`. `refs/remotes/origin/master` is still `ae408a8b`. So the first diagnosis's `sync` was carried out, and the refusal came back the same.

That diagnosis also blamed GitHub's CI gate. Master's `.agentic/stack.yaml:99-105` refutes it: `ci:` is commented out ("Off while GitHub Actions is blocked by billing"). Merges are decided by the local checks plus the open findings, and local `web-e2e` is off (`:22-25`).

What I could not read: the merge gate's own refusal message and the PR's check runs. `git`, `gh` and the SQLite state were not readable in this role. The cause above comes from the task record's findings, settlements and refusal history, which agree with each other.

### What a fresh run must do differently

- **Human first.** This is the second diagnosis. Look at the six `PM: final validation` findings against `7a00e04b` and `docs/evidence/GH-376/after/*.png`. They are met in substance. Settle them in the record with that evidence; no new code is needed for them. While they stay open, every sync, re-review or empty commit is refused the same way. Do not repeat the empty "record the state" commits (`24ebec1a`, `ed619550`).
- **Decide the one open plan conflict in the same step.** The finding at `web/src/mobile-standard/statesSales.ts:75` says the `loading` and `copy-failed` `WalkState` entries were dropped, and `docs/product/sales.md:409` says none are added. §10 says new walk states must be added, and its offender lists cannot take a new screen name's cells. The request asks for the states, and nobody with authority has decided. Do not quietly re-add the states, and do not grow the offender lists to make room for them.
- **If this is rebuilt from the issue**, keep the three subtasks: data, then renderer, then button. Also:
  - The shell height fix (`.disposicion .pantalla { min-height: 0; flex: 1 }`) and the delisting of the offender cells it frees go in one diff from the start.
  - `touch-action` is scoped to `.sale-bill > .boton`, not the shared `.boton`.
  - `Boton` gets no free `className`.
  - Any PM or UI acceptance criterion should be recorded against a file or a check, so a review can close it.
- Do not loosen `compare.sh`, `screens.json` or any offender list to get past the gate.
